We evaluated three API models across four physician-authored, multi-turn vignettes under baseline and entry-to-care instruction conditions, yielding 24 fixed-script transcripts; two cases also used adaptive standardized-patient simulation, yielding 12 transcripts. Self-care or home-management advice before any patient answer appeared in 9 of 12 baseline case-model cells and 0 of 12 instruction cells, while structured handoff summaries appeared in 0 of 12 and 10 of 12 cells, respectively. The instruction changed sequencing and documentation, although it did not reliably ensure elicitation of decisive facts. The preformulation gap should therefore be evaluated directly through observable first-contact behavior rather than inferred from diagnostic accuracy or final-answer quality.

</details>


### [163] [TileMix: Tile-Centric Mixed-Precision Attention for LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17336)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TileMix 是一个针对大语言模型长上下文预填充阶段的分块精度路由内核，将数值精度作为可执行的空间决策分发到硬件对齐的注意分数块上，通过FP16/INT8混合精度路径在融合密集注意力内保持密集token连接，无需训练即可恢复长上下文质量并提升预填充吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型长上下文预填充阶段因密集自注意力的二次方查询-键分数计算产生巨大计算和内存开销。现有方法要么使用统一的低精度路径，要么选择token交互，两者都未能在融合密集注意力内部对硬件对齐的分数块进行空间精度路由，缺乏在精度与效率间可控权衡的手段。

Method: TileMix将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的分数块（score tiles），将路由决策打包为紧凑位掩码（bitmasks），每个tile组通过FP16或INT8两条路径之一计算分数，并共享在线softmax状态更新。通过可扩展的精度分组，每个路由位可管理多个相邻key块，在长上下文中保持硬件对齐的计算块和紧凑元数据。TileMix路由所有合法tile组以保持密集token连接，无需训练，支持分组查询注意力、可变长度批次和INT8键/值缓存。

Result: 在LongEval、LV-Eval和A100预填充基准上，对LLaMA、Qwen和Vicuna模型族进行测试，TileMix恢复了在统一INT8精度下损失的长上下文质量，同时相比FP16提升了预填充吞吐量，实现了跨模型族的可控精度-效率权衡边界。

Conclusion: TileMix证明了在融合密集注意力中将数值精度作为空间可执行决策是可行且有效的，能够在不进行训练的情况下实现长上下文质量与预填充效率的可控平衡，为大规模LLM推理提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: Long-context prefill in large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory traffic because dense self-attention computes quadratic query-key scores. Existing methods either use a uniform low-precision path or select token interactions, leaving spatial precision routing over hardware-aligned score tiles outside fused dense attention. We introduce TileMix, a tile-centric precision-routing kernel that makes numerical precision an executable spatial decision over score-tile groups within fused dense attention. TileMix partitions the attention matrix into hardware-aligned score tiles, packs routing decisions into compact bitmasks, and dispatches each tile group through FP16 or INT8 score computation while both paths update a shared online-softmax state. Scalable precision grouping lets each routing bit govern multiple adjacent key tiles, preserving hardware-aligned compute tiles and compact metadata at long contexts. By routing all legal tile groups, TileMix preserves dense token connectivity, requires no training, and supports grouped-query attention, variable-length batches, and INT8 key/value caches. Across LongEval, LV-Eval, and A100 prefill benchmarks on LLaMA, Qwen, and Vicuna, TileMix recovers long-context quality lost under uniform INT8 and improves prefill throughput over FP16, yielding a controllable accuracy-efficiency frontier across model families. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileMix.

</details>


### [164] [LLM-Only PDDL Domain Repair with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17341)
*Nader Karimi Bavandpour,Pascal Bercher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了开放权重大语言模型在PDDL模型自动修复任务中的表现。实验表明，最佳LLM（高推理努力）的F1分数达到0.87，显著优于符号基线的0.49，但平均测试通过率仅0.82，在Thoughtful域上低至0.06，即便包含测试轨迹的最佳设置也仅达0.92，说明当前开放权重模型无法保证满足自动模型修复所需的测试约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI规划依赖PDDL领域模型的准确性，但用户提供的模型中可能存在错误。尽管已有自动化修复方法，研究者希望评估近期开放权重的大语言模型是否能以纯LLM方式完成PDDL模型的检测与修复任务，以探索LLM在此类符号推理任务中的潜力。

Method: 采用LLM-only方法，让开放权重的大语言模型直接对含有错误的PDDL模型进行修复，以用户提供的正测试计划（成功方案）和负测试计划（执行失败方案）作为约束条件。实验比较了符号基线方法（F1=0.49）与不同推理努力水平下的LLM表现。

Result: 最佳LLM在高推理努力下F1分数达0.87，比符号基线绝对提升0.38。但该设置平均测试通过率仅0.82，在Thoughtful域上仅为0.06；即使在包含测试轨迹的最佳设置下，平均测试通过率也只达到0.92。

Conclusion: 尽管开放权重LLM在修复准确率上大幅超越符号基线，但测试通过率不足意味着当前模型无法可靠地保证修复后的模型满足用户给定的测试约束，因此尚不足以支撑可靠的全自动模型修复。

Abstract: AI planning is concerned with finding a sequence of actions that achieves a specified goal. It relies on explicit models of the world, commonly represented in the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL). An active line of research investigates how errors in such models can be detected and repaired. For example, users may provide positive test plans that are solutions, and negative test plans that fail during execution. Automated repair methods then modify the PDDL model to satisfy these constraints. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of recent open-weight large language models to perform this repair task using an LLM-only approach. Our experiments show that the symbolic baseline achieves an $F_1$ score of $.49$, while the best-performing LLM reaches $.87$ with high reasoning effort, an absolute improvement of $.38$. However, that setting has a mean test pass rate of only $.82$, falling to $.06$ on the Thoughtful domain; even the best setting that includes the test traces reaches only $.92$. Thus, current open-weight models cannot guarantee satisfaction of the test constraints required for reliable automated model repair.

</details>


### [165] [Cognitive Graph Intelligence for Adaptive and Robust DDoS Attack Detection in Next Generation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17352)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Yeahia Sarker,Md Jafrin Hossain,Most. Humayra Khanom Rime,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphGAN，一种基于图的生成对抗网络，作为认知DDoS检测引擎。它通过滑动窗口将连续流量转换为k近邻图以保留特征相似性和时间依赖，用生成器合成少数类攻击样本解决类别不平衡，用基于GCN的判别器区分真假图数据，并训练独立的GCN分类器进行最终检测。在四个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的准确率、精确率和召回率，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击威胁网络可用性，需要一个能够感知流量、推断意图并支持自适应响应的认知检测过程。该方法面临严重的类别不平衡和非平稳条件，现有方法难以有效建模流之间的结构化关系和协同攻击行为，因此需要一种既能捕获流量间图结构关系又能缓解类不平衡的检测框架。

Method: 1) 使用滑动窗口将序列流量转换为k近邻图，保留特征相似性和时序依赖关系。2) 构建GraphGAN框架：生成器学习DDoS攻击分布并合成逼真的少数类样本，基于图卷积网络(GCN)的判别器区分真实与合成图数据。3) 在平衡后的数据集上训练独立的GCN分类器执行最终检测决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，GraphGAN在准确率、精确率和召回率方面均优于现有最先进方法，尤其在数据稀缺场景下优势明显。该方法有效建模了协同攻击行为并缓解了类别不平衡问题。

Conclusion: GraphGAN通过整合时序图构建、对抗性数据增强和GCN分类，为数据受限环境下的人侵检测提供了一种鲁棒且具备拓扑感知能力的解决方案，有效缓解了类别不平衡并建模了协调攻击行为。

Abstract: Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks threaten network availability, requiring a cognitive detection process that senses traffic, infers intent, and supports an adaptive response under severe class imbalance and non-stationary conditions. This paper proposes a Graph-based Generative Adversarial Network (GraphGAN) that serves as the cognitive detection engine for this task. GraphGAN captures the relational structure among traffic flows while addressing imbalance through adversarial generation of synthetic samples. Sequential flows are converted into $k$-nearest neighbor graphs using sliding windows to preserve feature-similarity and temporal dependencies among flows. The generator learns the distribution of DDoS attacks to synthesize realistic minority samples, while a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN)-based discriminator distinguishes real from synthetic graph data. A separate GCN classifier, trained on the balanced dataset, performs the final detection decision. Evaluations on four benchmark datasets show that GraphGAN achieves superior accuracy, precision, and recall compared to state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in data-scarce scenarios. By integrating temporal graph construction, adversarial augmentation, and GCN classification, GraphGAN effectively models coordinated attack behaviors and mitigates class imbalance, providing a robust and topology-aware solution for intrusion detection in data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [166] [LEGO-RL: Harness-Native Reinforcement Learning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17393)
*Yiming Du,Yuxin Jiang,Tao Yuan,Jianbo Dai,Shaowei Wang,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Xianzhi Yu,Lifeng Shang,Kam-Fai Wong,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEGO-RL是一个用于编码智能体的强化学习框架，通过三个核心支柱（忠实优化、可靠执行、可观测训练）桥接原生编码智能体环境与可扩展的策略梯度优化，在不修改内部控制流的情况下，在三个原生编码智能体环境中显著提升了Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在SWE-bench Verified上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的强化学习依赖长时间运行的智能体框架来管理工具集成、仓库上下文和执行反馈，但这些框架的原生执行环境与策略梯度训练存在固有错配：环境崩溃和奖励黑客行为会污染结果信号，训练-推理差异会导致行为轨迹与策略更新脱节。

Method: LEGO-RL基于三个支柱构建：(1) 忠实优化：通过进程内LLM代理捕获原始生成流，实现token级对齐和稳健的训练端log-probability重计算，即使在框架端压缩或重新序列化下也能工作；(2) 可靠执行：可扩展的沙箱编排，配备镜像缓存和分阶段防御机制以缓解奖励黑客行为；(3) 可观测训练：集成插件自动化验证和监控，配合Live UI提供细粒度轨迹诊断。

Result: 使用GSPO训练稀疏MoE模型Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，在三个原生编码智能体框架上均有显著提升：OpenHands SDK从64.0%提升至70.4%，Claude Code从62.4%提升至68.2%，OpenCode从57.2%提升至66.6%（SWE-bench Verified），同时保持rollout-训练概率相关性高于0.99。

Conclusion: LEGO-RL成功证明了在不修改原生编码智能体框架内部控制流的前提下，可以实现忠实、可靠且可观测的策略梯度优化训练，有效解决了原生执行环境与策略梯度训练之间的错配问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for coding agents increasingly relies on long-running agent harnesses to manage tool integration, repository contexts, and execution feedback. However, the native execution environments of these harnesses are inherently misaligned with policy-gradient training: environmental crashes and reward hacking corrupt outcome signals, while train-inference discrepancies decouple rollout behavior from policy updates. To address this, we present LEGO-RL, a framework that bridges native coding-agent harnesses with scalable policy-gradient optimization without modifying their internal control flow. LEGO-RL is built upon three pillars: (1) faithful optimization via in-process LLM proxying that captures raw generation streams for token-level alignment and robust trainer-side log-probability recomputation, even under harness-side compaction or re-serialization; (2) reliable execution via scalable sandbox orchestration featuring image caching and stage-wise defenses to mitigate reward hacking; and (3) observable training through an integrated plugin that automates validation and monitoring, paired with a Live UI for granular trajectory diagnostics. We evaluate LEGO-RL by training the sparse MoE model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with GSPO across three native coding-agent harnesses. LEGO-RL improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across OpenHands SDK (64.0% to 70.4%), Claude Code (62.4% to 68.2%), and OpenCode (57.2% to 66.6%) on SWE-bench Verified, while maintaining a rollout-training probability correlation above 0.99.

</details>


### [167] [Task-Aware Harness Provisioning for LLM Agents in Mission-Critical Infrastructure Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17433)
*Liangtao Lin,Qingang Zhang,Zhaomeng Zhu,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体（LLM agent）的harness配置优化建模为资源匹配问题，通过任务分类与harness分级来构建"任务→harness"映射，并提出"map-guided escalation"算法，在保持或提升任务精度的同时降低资源消耗。实验在液冷和电网两类关键基础设施任务上验证了该方法，发现harness配置遵循领域相关的精度-成本帕累托前沿，而非存在通用最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在关键基础设施上运行时依赖harness决定其可访问的信息、可用工具和可执行动作。现有系统通常对所有任务暴露相同的全面harness，这既不必要也造成资源浪费。因此，作者希望识别最优的harness配置，以解决"任务所需"与"harness所供"之间的资源匹配问题。

Method: 1) 基于底层系统的数学表示对关键基础设施任务进行分类；2) 按harness提供的信息类型和数量对其配置进行排序；3) 通过挖掘研究文献和测量受控智能体执行两个渠道构建任务到harness的映射；4) 提出map-guided escalation算法：从任务特定的最小harness出发，仅在自检失败时扩展到完整harness。

Result: 在液冷任务中，该方法将智能体准确率从全量配置下的0.652提升至0.715，并在与Reflexion精度相当的情况下节省48%的token；在电网任务中，全量harness仍保持精度最优，但基于映射的配置提供了更低成本的替代方案。

Conclusion: Harness配置不存在通用的最优方案，而是遵循领域相关的精度-成本帕累托前沿。不同任务领域需要不同的harness配置策略，在精度与成本之间进行权衡时需根据具体领域特征选择适当的配置。

Abstract: LLM agents have been widely adopted to operate mission-critical infrastructure (MCI). These agents normally rely on a harness that determines what information they can access, which tools they can use, and what actions they can take. Existing systems often expose the same comprehensive harness to every task, which may not be necessary and cause resource wastes. In this paper, we focus on the identification of optimal harness configurations, and view it as a resource-matching problem between what each task requires and what the harness provides. To measure this match, we classify MCI tasks based on the mathematical representation of the underlying system and rank harness configurations by the amount and type of information they provide. We then construct task-to-harness mappings from two sources: mining research literature and measuring controlled agent execution. Leveraging the measured mapping, we propose a new harness provisioning algorithm: map-guided escalation. It begins with a task-specific harness and expands to full provision only after a failed self-check. We evaluate our method in two representative MCI tasks: in liquid cooling, it improves the agent accuracy from 0.652 under full provision to 0.715 and achieves accuracy comparable to Reflexion with 48% fewer tokens; In power grids, full provision remains accuracy-optimal, while map-based provisioning offers lower-cost alternatives. These findings show that harness provisioning follows a domain-dependent accuracy-cost Pareto frontier rather than a universal optimum.

</details>


### [168] [Depth Enables Local Entropy: Quadratic Depth Dependence in Deep Variation-Norm ReLU Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17434)
*Tao Jiang,Minbo Gao,Shaowei Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Parhi-Nowak深度RBV²架构在Gaussian回归下的极小极大风险，通过构建局部packing证明了深度依赖是二次的（L²），从而与已知下界相比消除了深度因子的差距，并与上限匹配至对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的Parhi-Nowak深度RBV²架构（参数规模O(Lw²)）的上下界相差一个深度因子L，作者希望确定真实的深度依赖强度——即该差距是分析技巧造成的还是架构固有的。

Method: 构建"局部packing"方法：通过偏置修正的有界系数逼近定理和"平衡放大"技术（将深度D的ReLU网络乘以q时，利用一个常数通道使每个系数仅增长q^(1/D)），构造log基数Ω(L²w²log w)的码本，码字位于O(λ)L²球内且两两Ω(λ)分离。通过向量值RBV²块的层和代价O(Dw²q^(1/D))，结合Gaussian Fano不等式得到半径显式的下界。

Result: 在A=B=R、σ∝R的设定下，极小极大风险下界为Ω(L²w²log(w)R²/n)；基于伪维度有限网的上界为Õ(L²w²R²/n)。上下界匹配至对数因子，证明极小极大风险对深度呈二次多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 该架构的极小极大风险对深度具有固有的二次依赖（非分析假象），在较小半径下出现由表示能力限制主导的转变行为。该结果闭合了Parhi-Nowak架构上下界之间的深度因子差距，深化了对RBV²架构统计复杂度的理解。

Abstract: We study Gaussian regression over the explicit vector-valued Parhi--Nowak deep-RBV^2 architecture with depth L, width w, layer-sum variation budget A, and output bound B. For this O(L w^2)-parameterized architecture, the known lower and upper bounds differ by one factor of depth. We construct a local packing showing that the quadratic depth dependence is intrinsic under an explicit sample-size-dependent radius condition. The packing has log-cardinality Omega(L^2 w^2 log w); its codewords lie in an O(lambda) L^2 ball and are pairwise Omega(lambda)-separated. The main ingredients are a bias-corrected bounded-coefficient approximation theorem and balanced amplification: multiplying a depth-D ReLU network by q can be implemented using one constant channel so that every coefficient grows by only q^(1/D). Translation to vector-valued RBV^2 blocks then has layer-sum cost O(D w^2 q^(1/D)). Gaussian Fano yields a radius-explicit lower bound governed by the output, testing, and representation scales. Under A=B=R, sigma proportional to R, and the stated radius condition, this gives minimax risk at least of order L^2 w^2 log(w) R^2/n. A pseudodimension-based finite-net upper bound gives O-tilde(L^2 w^2 R^2/n) for unbounded Gaussian responses. Thus the minimax risk has quadratic polynomial dependence on depth, up to logarithmic factors, and exhibits a transition to representation-limited behavior at smaller radius.

</details>


### [169] [Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model for Faithful Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17443)
*Xingrui Zhuo,Jiapu Wang,Manzong Huang,Gongqing Wu,Xindong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIRLM（Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model），一种结合结构内化规则生成与LLM的知识图谱推理方法，通过SIRG生成器、结构不变性KG分词器和基于规则约束消息传播的神经符号推理器，解决LLM在KG推理中"推理证据感知漂移"问题，在36个数据集上显著优于17个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在KG推理任务中虽表现优异，但KG结构上下文与LLM参数化知识之间存在表征不一致性问题，导致LLM无法有效感知符合KG约束的推理证据，既影响推理有效性也影响推理忠实度。作者将这一问题定义为"LLM在KG上的推理证据感知漂移"（reasoning evidence perception drift）。

Method: SIRLM以结构规则生成为核心，耦合结构知识的参数化学习与推理逻辑的忠实度评估。具体包含三个关键组件：(1) Structure-Internalized Rule Generator (SIRG)：带结构关系记忆的上下文学习模块，协调结构知识与参数知识；(2) KG tokenizer：基于结构不变性学习，提供可学习的结构表示；(3) neuro-symbolic reasoner：基于规则约束的消息传播，提供忠实的规则执行反馈。SIRLM可无缝集成到SFT和GRPO等标准LLM训练范式中。

Result: 在36个数据集上与17个SOTA KG推理方法进行对比实验，SIRLM展现出显著的性能优越性。

Conclusion: 通过结构内化规则生成的核心设计，SIRLM有效缓解了LLM在KG推理中的证据感知漂移问题，使LLM能够紧密锚定于KG验证的推理证据，在提升推理有效性的同时保证了推理的忠实度，且兼容现有LLM主流训练范式。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Reasoning (KGR) aims to discover latent facts by leveraging the structural evidence available in KGs, posing a challenge to the structural semantic understanding capability of KGR models. Recent studies have demonstrated that Large Language Models (LLMs) can achieve remarkable progress on KGR tasks via flexible in-context learning. However, the inherent representation inconsistency between KG structural context and LLM parametric knowledge remains inadequately addressed. This limitation prevents LLMs from effectively perceiving reasoning evidence that aligns with KG constraints, which undermines both the effectiveness and faithfulness of reasoning. We refer to this problem as reasoning evidence perception drift of LLMs over KGs. To address this problem, we propose a Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model (SIRLM), which centers on structural rule generation to couple the parametric learning of structural knowledge with the faithfulness evaluation of reasoning logic, enabling LLMs to anchor tightly to KG-grounded evidence. Specifically, we first design a Structure-Internalized Rule Generator (SIRG), which incorporates an in-context learning block augmented with a structural relation memory to coordinate structural and parametric knowledge. Furthermore, we equip SIRG with a KG tokenizer based on structural invariance learning and a neuro-symbolic reasoner based on rule-constrained message propagation. These components provide SIRG with learnable structural representations and faithful rule-execution feedback, respectively. Our SIRLM can be seamlessly integrated into standard LLM training paradigms, such as SFT and GRPO. Extensive experiments against 17 state-of-the-art KGR methods on 36 datasets demonstrate the significant superiority of SIRLM.

</details>


### [170] [SAGE: Self-Evolving Storyboard Skills via Attribution-Guided Rule Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17468)
*Maolin Ran,Xiaoyang Lu,Jiaqi Liu,Jian Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE是一个已部署的框架，用于从专家演示中学习、归因、演化并路由导演知识，以自动化短剧故事板的生成。通过对比训练剧本与专家故事板提取内容无关的规则，结合生成记录与局部反馈进行规则迭代更新，并按场景打包规则实现限定检索。在18个测试片段上得分77.8（专家验证的评分标准），专业导演得分为77.1；在虚拟电影工作室部署14天，生成1,344个叙事组输出，87.2%无需实质修改即被接受，作者时间减少83%以上。同时发布了首个配对剧本与专业导演故事板的数据集PROSE（68个片段）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业故事板制作依赖隐性的导演专业知识，是工业瓶颈。用大语言模型自动化的关键在于提供导演知识，但面临三个挑战：知识获取（技艺隐含在范例中或需手工编写）、知识精炼（已有知识未对照执行结果评估，且不透明生成导致无法将反馈归因到具体决策知识）、知识注入（全部注入超出上下文，逐叙事组手动选择不可扩展）。

Method: SAGE框架包含四个阶段：(1) 学习——通过对比每个训练剧本与其专家故事板，推导出与剧情内容无关的导演规则；(2) 归因——生成过程中记录每个叙事组采纳的规则，将局部反馈与具体规则关联；(3) 演化——结合归因记录与局部反馈对个别规则进行定向更新；(4) 路由——演化后的规则形成场景包并配备路由索引，每个叙事组只检索适合其情境的有限规则集，无需专家干预。

Result: 在3种体裁共18个测试片段上，SAGE获得77.8分（专家验证的评分标准），超过专业导演的77.1分；部署于虚拟工作室14天，生成1,344个叙事组输出，87.2%被接受且无需实质修改，制作团队每集作者时间减少83%以上；发布了包含68个片段的PROSE数据集。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了通过归因引导的演化机制可以有效解决导演知识获取、精炼与注入三大挑战，使自动化故事板生成达到甚至超过专业导演水平，并在实际生产中显著提升效率，展示了该方法在专业影视制作中的实用价值。

Abstract: Storyboards turn screenplays into visual shot plans for automated short drama production. Professional storyboarding relies on tacit directorial expertise and remains an industrial bottleneck. Large language models can automate this step, but methods for supplying directing knowledge face three challenges: (1) Knowledge acquisition: the craft remains implicit in exemplars or must be written manually. (2) Knowledge refinement: authored knowledge is not evaluated against execution outcomes, and opaque generation prevents feedback attribution to the knowledge behind each decision. (3) Knowledge injection: injecting all knowledge exceeds usable context, while manual selection for every narrative group does not scale. We present SAGE (Skill with Attribution-Guided Evolution), a deployed framework that learns, attributes, evolves, and routes directing knowledge from expert demonstrations. SAGE derives rules that are independent of episode content by contrasting each training screenplay with its expert storyboard. During generation, the model records each narrative group's adopted rules. Combining these records with localized feedback enables targeted updates to individual rules. Evolved rules form scenario packages with a routing index, so each group retrieves only a bounded set appropriate to its situation without expert intervention. On 18 test episodes across three genres, SAGE scored 77.8 on a rubric validated by experts, versus 77.1 for professional directors. Deployed for 14 days on Virtual Film Studio, SAGE produced 1,344 narrative group outputs; 87.2 percent were accepted without substantive edits, and the production team recorded over 83 percent less authoring time per episode. We release PROSE, the first public dataset pairing screenplays with storyboards by professional directors across 68 episodes: https://github.com/creDreams/PROSE.

</details>


### [171] [When AI Designs AI: Innovation or Imitation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17471)
*Yikang Yang,Zhengxin Yang,Luzhou Peng,Minghao Luo,Yanqi Kan,Wanling Gao,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套分析框架，将人类设计的算法方法和LLM智能体设计的算法方法映射到任务特定的算法设计空间中，并在模块级别量化二者的算法差异。实验表明，当前LLM智能体偶尔能匹配或超越人类SOTA性能（72个配置中10个），但96.8%的智能体设计方法仍然落在人类衍生的算法设计空间内，近半数完全匹配已有的人类算法设计，说明智能体主要以重组和复用人类算法选择为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体设计方法能力的增强，人们需要回答两个核心问题：智能体设计的方法与人类设计的方法相比性能如何，以及二者的算法设计在本质上有多大的差异。

Method: 提出一种分析框架：(1)从人类设计的方法中推导出任务特定的算法设计空间；(2)将人类设计和智能体设计的方法映射到这些空间中；(3)在模块层面量化二者的算法差异。在涵盖多种模态的代表性开放AI任务套件上评估多种广泛使用的LLM智能体。

Result: 智能体在72个配置中有10个能匹配或超越人类SOTA性能，但该成功在不同任务和智能体间不能可靠泛化；96.8%的智能体设计方法落在人类衍生的算法设计空间内，近一半（约50%）完全匹配已有的人类算法设计。

Conclusion: 尽管当前LLM智能体偶尔可以达到或超越人类SOTA性能，它们的算法设计仍然处于人类衍生的算法设计空间内，显示出对已有算法选择的复用和重组，而非真正意义上的算法创新。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have made them increasingly capable of designing methods for complex AI tasks. This raises two central questions about agent-designed methods relative to human-designed methods: how well they perform, and how different their algorithmic designs are. To study these questions, this paper introduces an analysis that derives task-specific algorithmic design spaces from human-designed methods, maps both human- and agent-designed methods into these spaces, and quantifies their algorithmic differences at the module level. Widely used LLM agents are evaluated on a suite of representative, open-ended AI tasks spanning multiple modalities, and the methods they design are analyzed in terms of both task performance and algorithmic differences from human-designed methods. Experimental results show that current agents can occasionally match or surpass human state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance (10/72 configurations), but such success does not generalize reliably across tasks or agents. Moreover, 96.8% of agent-designed methods fall within human-derived algorithmic design spaces, largely recombining algorithmic choices found in human-designed methods, while nearly half exactly match an existing human algorithmic design. Taken together, these findings suggest that although current agents can occasionally match or surpass human SOTA performance, their algorithmic designs remain within human-derived algorithmic design spaces, reflecting the reuse and recombination of algorithmic choices.

</details>


### [172] [Towards Better Agents for Multi-Turn User Interaction: The Next User Turn Is More Than Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17499)
*Yiwen Zhao,Zhihao Wen,Yuchen Mao,Mingxuan Jiang,Yihao Hu,Pan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈感知的信用分配方法（FACA），利用对话中用户下一轮的反应作为局部时序信号，为多轮用户交互智能体的强化学习提供更细粒度的信用分配，在不引入额外评论网络或rollout的前提下，比仅依赖最终结果奖励的Interactive GRPO基线在九个领域平均提升5.91（8B）和10.22（14B）个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向用户的工具代理需要在多轮对话中协调对话与工具使用，但现有的交互式强化学习通常将整个rollout压缩为单一终端奖励，无法区分有效提问、错误发生和后续修复等不同环节的贡献。作者认为下一轮用户反应不仅是上下文，更是关于前一轮交互质量的含噪局部证据，应当被更精细地利用来改进信用分配。

Method: FACA将每一个用户反应与对应的对话片段对齐，计算局部归一化的反应优势（reaction advantage），并将其与经过验证的终端结果优势（terminal outcome advantage）相加。该方法不需要额外的critic网络，也不需要额外的rollout，直接利用对话中已有的用户反应信号。

Result: 在模拟器、可见对话、初始化、rollout和优化设置均匹配的outcome-only Interactive GRPO对照下，FACA在九个领域的τ-family平均指标上，8B和14B模型分别提升5.91和10.22个百分点（基于三次独立训练的平均）。增益主要集中在Telecom领域；在8B模型上随机化反应极性会消除Telecom增益。同一排序在Pare-Bench和Co-Gym的零样本测试中保持一致。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，下一轮用户反应能够为改进多轮用户交互智能体提供可操作、有信息量的局部信用信号，验证了利用用户即时反馈进行更细粒度信用分配的可行性。

Abstract: User-facing tool agents must coordinate dialogue and tool use as user goals unfold over multiple turns. Yet interactive reinforcement learning typically reduces each rollout to a terminal reward, assigning the same credit to effective elicitation, errors, and later repair. The next user turn is more than context: it also provides noisy, temporally local evidence about the preceding user-to-user segment. We introduce \textbf{F}eedback-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{C}redit \textbf{A}ssignment (\textsc{FACA}), which aligns each reaction with that segment, derives a locally normalized reaction advantage, and adds it to verified terminal outcome advantage without an extra critic or rollout. Against an outcome-only Interactive GRPO control matched in simulator, visible dialogue, initialization, rollout, and optimization, \textsc{FACA} improves the nine-domain $τ$-family average across three independently trained runs by 5.91 and 10.22 percentage points at 8B and 14B, respectively. Gains concentrate in Telecom; at 8B, randomizing reaction polarity removes the Telecom gain. The same ordering holds zero-shot on Pare-Bench and Co-Gym. These results demonstrate that next-turn user reactions provide actionable local credit for improving multi-turn user-interacting agents.

</details>


### [173] [SGHA: Evidence-Grounded Research Problem Discovery with Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17501)
*Sarvesh Gharat,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化缺口假设智能体（SGHA），一个完全自动化的、以语料库为先的研究问题发现系统，完全在本地开源9B语言模型上运行，无需依赖专有前沿模型API。SGHA将文献语料构建为证据链接的论文对象和类型化证据图，检测未解决的结构性模式，筛选候选研究缺口，并生成可追溯的研究问题家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI科学家系统在早期研究问题形成阶段严重依赖专有前沿模型，这些模型的知识是不透明的黑箱，导致研究问题的证据基础难以审计，易受模型特定幻觉和偏见影响；同时将专有研究材料传输到外部API还会引发机密性、隐私和数据治理方面的担忧。

Method: SGHA包含四个核心步骤：(1) 将科学文献语料结构化为证据链接的论文对象和类型化证据图；(2) 检测跨论文未解决的结构性模式；(3) 在表述之前筛选候选缺口；(4) 生成可追溯的研究问题家族，输出假设、目标、成功标准和剩余歧义。所有基于LLM的组件均使用本地服务的开源9B语言模型执行。

Result: 在五个机器学习领域中将SGHA与AI Scientist-v2的想法生成模块进行了比较，结果表明显式的语料库结构和证据约束推理能够在无需依赖前沿模型进行生成或验证的情况下，支持有前景且可检查的研究问题表述。

Conclusion: 显式的语料库结构和证据约束推理可以替代专有前沿模型，实现可审计、可追溯且具有较好前景的自动化研究问题发现。

Abstract: Recent efforts toward fully automated AI scientists have demonstrated that language-model agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft scientific manuscripts. However, during the early stages of research, when research problems are formulated, these AI scientists often rely heavily on proprietary frontier models. Their proposals are shaped by opaque parametric knowledge and by literature searches conditioned on the proposals themselves. Such knowledge is effectively a black box, and this dependence makes the evidential basis and validity of generated research problems difficult to audit and leaves the process vulnerable to model-specific hallucinations and biases. Furthermore, if proprietary research materials are transmitted to external APIs, the use of these models creates confidentiality, privacy, and data-governance concerns.
  We introduce the Structural Gap Hypothesis Agent (SGHA), a fully automated, corpus-first research-problem discovery system that runs entirely on a local LLM. SGHA structures a scientific literature corpus into evidence-linked paper objects and a typed evidence graph, detects unresolved structural patterns across papers, screens candidate gaps before formulation, and produces traceable research-problem families. In particular, it is able to output assumptions, objectives, success criteria, and remaining ambiguities. All LLM-based components of SGHA are executed using a locally served open-weight 9B language model, without requiring proprietary frontier-model APIs. We compare SGHA with the AI Scientist-v2 idea formulation module in five machine-learning domains. Our results suggest that explicit corpus structure and evidence-constrained reasoning can support promising, inspectable research-problem formulation without relying on frontier models during generation or verification.

</details>


### [174] [Agent Lightning v1.0: Towards Harnessed Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17528)
*Zhiyuan He,Siwei Zhang,Zhiwen Zhou,Yuqing Yang,Yu Kang,Yuge Zhang,Luna K. Qiu,Tin Yan Tsui,Jiahang Xu,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Agent Lightning v1.0，一个约3500行代码的轻量级框架，用于"harnessed agentic RL"（驯服式智能体强化学习）——即部署时使用的agent harness直接参与模型后训练的新范式。该框架支持任意agent harness，在指令跟随、搜索和编码等任务上验证了有效性，仅用6K训练样本即将Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上的准确率从41.8%提升到56.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代agent运行在harness中，harness负责管理工具、上下文和控制流，已成为agent系统的关键组成部分。传统智能体强化学习将训练引擎作为环境交互循环的所有者，但在harnessed agentic RL中，部署时的harness直接参与模型后训练，训练器只能观察到LLM请求-响应对序列，这带来了重分词、样本合并、优势计算、损失归一化和后端调度等一系列新挑战，需要系统性的研究和框架支持。

Method: Agent Lightning v1.0采用解耦架构，通过LLM端点代理将任意agent与RL训练连接（该架构已被verl Uni-Agent、AReaL 2.0、slime、Polar等框架采用）。harness拥有环境交互循环，训练器仅从请求-响应对序列中进行学习。框架约3500行代码，支持任意agent harness，并提供完整的可复现编码agent RL流水线。

Result: 在指令跟随、搜索和编码三类agent上进行评估。在编码任务中，仅使用6K训练样本和适度计算资源，RL训练将Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上的性能从41.8%提升至56.4%，实现了14.6个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: Agent Lightning v1.0验证了harnessed agentic RL作为新范式的可行性，通过轻量级框架和完整开源工作流，为研究该范式下的重分词、样本合并、优势计算、损失归一化等关键挑战提供了实用的测试平台，推动了可复现的agentic RL研究。

Abstract: Modern agents operate inside agent harnesses that manage tools, context, and control flow, making the harness a critical part of the agent system. Our original Agent Lightning introduced a disaggregated architecture that connects arbitrary agents to RL training through an LLM endpoint proxy, an approach later adopted by frameworks such as verl Uni-Agent, AReaL 2.0, slime, and Polar. We refer to this paradigm as harnessed agentic RL, where the deploy-time harness directly participates in model post-training. Harnessed agentic RL differs fundamentally from traditional agentic RL: the harness, rather than the training engine, owns the environment interaction loop, while the trainer observes only sequences of LLM request-response pairs. This introduces challenges in retokenization, sample merging, advantage calculation, loss normalization, and backend scheduling, which can substantially affect training stability and effectiveness. We present Agent Lightning v1.0, a lightweight framework for harnessed agentic RL implemented in approximately 3,500 lines of code. It supports arbitrary agent harnesses and serves as a practical testbed for studying these challenges. We evaluate it on instruction-following, search, and coding agents, and provide a complete reproducible pipeline for coding-agent RL. Using only 6K training examples and modest compute, RL improves Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified from 41.8% to 56.4%, a 14.6-point absolute gain. We release the complete workflow and training scripts to facilitate reproducible research on harnessed agentic RL.

</details>


### [175] [When to Review: Spaced Repetition for Continual Pre-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17530)
*Alankar Atreya,Devesh Batra,Yoages Kumar Mantri,Geremy Bantug,Greig A Cowan,Raad Khraishi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spaced Repetition Training (SRT)，一个受认知科学启发的持续预训练框架，利用SuperMemo-2算法进行自适应复习调度，根据每个样本的遗忘速度安排回放，而非全局统一混合采样。在时间分离的Wikipedia和代码语料上，SRT恢复了朴素持续预训练丢失的5-37个百分点的旧知识精度，同时保持或提升新知识获取，并在更大规模上保持广泛基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的持续预训练需要在获取新信息的同时不遗忘旧知识。现有回放方法通常选择全局固定的新旧数据混合比例并均匀采样，忽略了不同样本的遗忘速度差异。这种"一刀切"策略无法针对性地巩固那些更容易被遗忘的样本，导致稳定性-可塑性权衡不够理想。

Method: SRT框架将持续预训练视为自适应复习调度问题，具体包括：1）基于认知科学中的间隔重复原则，采用SuperMemo-2算法进行样例复习调度；2）维护每个样本的复习状态；3）将每个样本的困惑度映射为回忆质量信号；4）对历史样例安排保留复习，对新样例安排巩固训练；5）不改变模型、目标函数和优化器，仅调整训练循环中的数据调度。

Result: 在时间分离的Wikipedia和代码语料上，SRT改善了稳定性-可塑性权衡：跨模型规模恢复了朴素持续预训练丢失的5到37个百分点的旧知识精度，同时保留或提升新知识获取。在更大规模上，SRT保持了广泛基准性能，而朴素持续预训练和均匀回放严重退化。在视觉和表格数据上的实验进一步表明，配合适当的回忆信号，该调度原则可扩展到语言领域之外。

Conclusion: 基于间隔重复的调度原则是一种高效且通用的持续预训练策略，通过针对性地回放易遗忘样本而非均匀采样，可以在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下显著改善稳定性-可塑性权衡，且该原则在多种数据模态上具有普适性。

Abstract: Continual pre-training of large language models must acquire new information without erasing old knowledge. Existing replay methods often choose a global old/new mixture and sample uniformly, ignoring that examples differ in how quickly they are forgotten. We formulate continual pre-training as adaptive review scheduling: the training loop should decide not only how much history to replay, but which examples should return at each step. We introduce Spaced Repetition Training (SRT), a continual learning framework inspired by cognitive science, which schedules sample-rehearsal using the SuperMemo-2 (SM-2) algorithm. SRT maintains per-example review state, maps per-example perplexity to a recall-quality signal, and schedules historical examples for retention and new examples for consolidation while leaving the model, objective, and optimizer unchanged. On temporally separated Wikipedia and code corpora, SRT improves the stability-plasticity trade-off, recovering 5 to 37 percentage points of old-knowledge accuracy lost by naive continual pre-training across model scales while preserving or improving new-knowledge acquisition. At larger scale, SRT preserves broad benchmark performance that naive continual pre-training and uniform replay substantially degrade. Experiments with vision and tabular data further suggest that the scheduling principle extends beyond language when paired with an appropriate recall signal.

</details>


### [176] [Quantifying Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: Belief-Dependent Robustness for Safe Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17574)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Kretinsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RATTL（风险对抗总奖励学习）将代理的谨慎程度与认知不确定性挂钩：代理对未知动态持有贝叶斯后验，并针对半径随该后验单调变化的Wasserstein模糊集进行规划。随着证据积累，模糊半径收缩，行为在最大鲁棒性与风险中性总奖励最大化之间连续插值。作者证明了该规划问题在瞬态与紧性条件下的适定性，并提出了“安全三明治”定理：RATTL价值介于未获信息的鲁棒值与完全知识最优值之间，且差距随后验集中而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际强化学习场景中，代理在探索环境时会面临不确定性，但传统的风险中性方法忽略了这种不确定性带来的风险。作者希望通过将谨慎程度与认知不确定性（即后验不确定性）联系起来，设计一种从风险规避到风险中性的连续行为过渡机制，使代理在不确定时保持谨慎、在信息充分时追求最优。

Method: 该方法基于熵风险价值（Entropic Value-at-Risk）对偶性，将风险等级的选择转化为模糊集半径的选择。代理维持对未知动态的贝叶斯后验，构建以该后验为中心的Wasserstein模糊集（半径是后验的单调函数），并在这个模糊集内进行对抗性规划（即最坏情况下的总奖励优化）。该方法还包含后验集中时模糊集收缩的机制，实现从鲁棒性到风险中性的连续插值。

Result: 作者证明了在瞬态和紧性条件下规划问题的适定性；提出了“安全三明治”定理，证明RATTL价值有上下界（介于未获信息鲁棒值与全知识最优值之间），且该界随后验集中而收缩至零。在经典二值风险设定中，该方法退化为条件风险价值（CVaR），其风险水平由后验熵决定。一个具体示例展示了代理在识别阈值达到之前会推迟高效行动。

Conclusion: RATTL为运行不确定性下的代理（包括基于LLM的系统）提供了以认知不确定性驱动的运行时安全保障机制，实现风险感知与最优性之间的动态平衡。该方法通过贝叶斯后验与Wasserstein模糊集的结合，从理论上保证了安全性，并在实践中展示了可解释的行为模式。

Abstract: How cautious should an agent be while it is still learning its environment? We propose RATTL (Risk-Adversarial Total-Reward Learning), which ties caution to epistemic uncertainty: the agent holds a Bayesian posterior over unknown dynamics and plans against a Wasserstein ambiguity set whose radius is a monotone function of that posterior. The radius contracts with evidence, so behaviour interpolates continuously between worst-case robustness and risk-neutral total-reward maximization. The design follows the duality underlying the Entropic Value-at-Risk, which converts the choice of a risk level into the choice of an ambiguity radius. We show the resulting planning problem is well posed under transience and compactness conditions, and prove a Safety Sandwich: the RATTL value lies between the uninformed robust value and the full- knowledge optimum, with a gap that vanishes as the posterior concentrates. In a canonical binary-hazard instance, the induced criterion reduces to Conditional Value-at-Risk at a level set by the posterior entropy. A worked example shows the agent deferring the efficient action until a sharp identification threshold. RATTL targets runtime safety for agents, including LLM-based systems, acting under uncertainty.

</details>


### [177] [TRUSS: Towards Task-Reliable and User-Safe Automated Agent Skill Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17588)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRUSS是一个基于证据引导的框架，用于自动生成既功能有效又安全可靠的Agent Skills。它通过静态审查验证功能声明和九项安全属性，再通过可控执行环境中影子代理的轨迹执行来暴露未被静态检查发现的失败行为，并利用执行证据迭代优化Skill生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成Agent Skills虽能提升任务性能，但仅从工件或最终结果评估无法揭示代理实际执行的动作及产生的副作用，导致功能失败和安全风险难以被静态检查捕获。

Method: TRUSS采用两阶段框架：(1) 静态门控阶段，检查功能声明与源/领域证据的对应关系，并对完整工件评估九种预定义安全属性；(2) 可控执行环境阶段，由影子代理加载候选Skill，通过代理工具暴露请求动作供策略强制执行，并记录带溯源保留的执行轨迹。功能失败和属性违规被回溯至具体Skill内容以指导迭代修复。

Result: 在168个SkillInject、155个SkillSafetyBench和187个SkillGenBench任务上，TRUSS实现100%的漏洞检测精确率和召回率。攻击成功率从38.71%降至19.35%（GPT 5.5）和从46.45%降至29.68%（GPT 5.4），零攻击回归。Skill生成任务效果从17.11%提升至52.94%，安全率从50.80%提升至100.00%。

Conclusion: 执行证据能够揭示工件审查遗漏的行为失败，并能引导Skill生成实现功能与安全双重验证，证明从"只看产物"到"看行为轨迹"的评估范式转变对Agent Skills的可靠生成至关重要。

Abstract: Agent Skills package reusable natural language procedures with executable resources, enabling software agents to acquire task specific capabilities without model adaptation. Automatically generating such Skills can improve task performance, yet evaluating a candidate solely from its artifact or final task outcome leaves unresolved which actions the equipped agent will perform and which side effects those actions will produce. We present TRUSS, an evidence guided framework for generating functionally effective and safety reliable Agent Skills. TRUSS first inspects functional claims against source and domain evidence while evaluating the complete artifact under nine predefined safety properties. Candidates admitted by this static gate are loaded by a shadow agent inside a Controllable Execution Environment, where brokered tools expose requested actions to policy enforcement and record their results as provenance preserving execution traces. Functional failures and property violations are linked back to the responsible Skill content and used to guide iterative refinement.
  We evaluate TRUSS on 168 SkillInject artifacts, 155 SkillSafetyBench cases, and all 187 tasks in SkillGenBench. TRUSS achieves 100.00\% precision and recall in vulnerability detection. Repair reduces attack success from 38.71\% to 19.35\% with GPT 5.5 and from 46.45\% to 29.68\% with GPT 5.4, with zero attack regression. For Skill generation, TRUSS raises task effectiveness from 17.11\% without Skills to 52.94\%, while increasing the benchmark Security rate from 50.80\% to 100.00\%. These results show that execution evidence can expose behavioral failures missed by artifact inspection and can guide Skill generation toward jointly verified functional and safety outcomes.

</details>


### [178] [MoNe: Modular Neural Memory for Efficient Long Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17616)
*Wonguk Cho,Kyubyung Chae,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy,Sunghyun Park,Hyoungwoo Park,Jeongho Kim,Arash Behboodi,Kyuwoong Hwang,Sungrack Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoNe是一种轻量级模块化神经记忆系统，可附加到任意冻结的预训练Transformer上，实现无需重新训练的长上下文推理。它通过测试时学习的快速权重神经记忆网络以固定大小片段读取上下文，在推理时仅由查询token生成键值，实现了O(N)预处理和O(1)查询成本，在128K token时相比上下文内学习（ICL）减少约80%的计算量和峰值GPU内存，仅增加6.4%参数开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer在长上下文场景下存在推理成本随上下文长度线性增长的问题，且预训练模型的原生上下文窗口有限。上下文内学习（ICL）虽然在长上下文中表现不错，但其计算和内存开销随N增长，亟需一种既能扩展上下文长度又不会带来高昂推理成本的解决方案。

Method: MoNe采用两阶段设计：(1) 预处理阶段，通过测试时学习将上下文按固定大小片段编码为快速权重神经记忆网络，使用层局部梯度更新；(2) 推理阶段，仅从查询token生成键和值，无需重读任何上下文token。这种两阶段设计将推理成本与上下文长度解耦。

Result: 在128K token场景下，MoNe相比ICL同时降低约80%的计算量和峰值GPU内存，仅增加6.4%的参数开销。在RULER基准的needle-in-a-haystack和词提取任务上，MoNe表现出强性能，且能泛化到远超骨干网络原生窗口的上下文长度，而ICL在此场景下性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: MoNe证明了一种可行且高效的长上下文扩展方案：通过模块化神经记忆附加在冻结的预训练模型上，以极低的参数和计算开销实现接近无限上下文长度的推理能力，且无需重新训练现有模型。

Abstract: We present MoNe, a lightweight modular neural memory that attaches to any frozen pretrained Transformer to enable long-context inference without retraining. MoNe reads context in fixed-size segments via test-time learning of fast-weight neural memory networks with layer-localized gradient updates; at inference, the memory generates keys and values from the query tokens alone, with no context tokens re-read. This two-phase design decouples inference cost from context length, achieving $O(N)$ preprocessing and $O(1)$ query cost with peak GPU memory that does not grow with $N$. At 128K tokens, MoNe reduces both compute and peak GPU memory by approximately 80% compared to ICL with only 6.4% parameter overhead. MoNe generalizes to context lengths far beyond the backbone's native window, achieving strong performance on needle-in-a-haystack and word extraction benchmarks from RULER, where ICL degrades sharply.

</details>


### [179] [Validated Adaptation for Aerial Crowd Monitoring at Mass Gathering Scale: A Deployment Protocol, a Severity Law, and a Diagnostic for Label-Free Drone Crowd Counting, Toward the FIFA World Cup 2034 (Saudi Arabia)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17625)
*AlAnoud AllGhayth,AlJawharh AlOtaibi,Jude AlSubaie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对沙特阿拉伯2034年世界杯及朝觐规模的无人机人群计数场景，提出并验证了一套无标签域自适应计数与风险预警方法。基于525次受控实验、全分辨率语料研究、五次证伪消融和五条件安全联锁评估，证明了标签无关自适应能恢复31%-49%的域偏移误差（最强方法比冻结源模型提升41.8 MAE，p=7.5e-10，d=2.52）。研究建立了区分方法的严重性定律和稳定性预算，发现适应信号主要由归一化而非流驱动（连续性残差对比例计数误差不变，r=0.999），并指出幅度门控会放弃58%的性能余量。最终提出无条件自适应+尾部监控的政策及六点协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机人群计数系统在实际部署中面临域偏移（如拍摄角度、光照、密度分布等不同于训练数据）导致精度显著下降的问题，且真实场景中无法依赖人工标签进行微调。在朝觐和世界杯等超大规模人群聚集场景中，须在密集人流形成踩踏前提供可靠预警，因此需要一种无需标签、能自动适应域偏移并具有安全联锁保证的计数方法。

Method: 研究采用"标签无关的自适应+尾部监控"策略框架。具体方法包括：(1) 对多种域偏移（四种损坏类型、五种严重程度）进行系统性受控实验（525次运行）；(2) 使用物理信息守恒先验（continuity equation）构造自适应信号，但通过消融实验将其分解为归一化驱动与流驱动成分；(3) 建立严重性定律和稳定性预算来区分不同方法的安全配置；(4) 采用无条件自适应（不依赖偏移幅度估计）+尾部（尾部风险/密度）监控作为最终政策；(5) 通过全分辨率语料验证真实空中视角下的+48 MAE差距及修复效果。

Result: 无标签自适应恢复31%-49%的域偏移误差；最强方法（连续性约束）相较冻结源模型获得41.8 MAE的提升（95% CI [34.1, 49.6]，p=7.5e-10，d=2.52）；全分辨率语料上源模型重训后验证MAE从约48降至14.6（改善34%），自适应有效修复了密集场景的低估；通量风险模块在6个完整片段中于2个真实拥堵片段触发预警；消融证实适应信号是归一化驱动而非流驱动（连续性残差对比例计数误差不变，相关性r=0.999，40%输入损坏仅使精度变化0.05 MAE）；幅度门控会放弃58%的性能余量。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：在真实人群安全场景中应采用"无条件自适应+尾部监控"作为部署政策，而非基于偏移幅度估计的门控策略。自适应机制主要依赖归一化补偿而非流体动力学约束，这一发现为后续改进指明了方向。作者最后给出了包含六点的部署协议，为后续实际应用（如沙特世界杯和朝觐管理）提供了可操作的指南。

Abstract: Saudi Arabia will host the 2034 FIFA World Cup and already operates crowd management at Hajj scale. Drone-based counting must hold accuracy on footage unlike anything in its training corpus, without labels, and must warn of dangerous inflow before a crush forms. We deliver a validated answer built on 525 controlled runs, a full-resolution corpus study, five falsification ablations, and a five-condition safety-interlock evaluation. Label-free adaptation recovers 31-49% of shift-induced error across four corruptions and five severities, with the strongest method gaining 41.8 MAE over the frozen source (95% CI [34.1, 49.6], p=7.5x10^-10, d=2.52). We establish a severity law separating methods with a constant absolute margin from the one whose margin grows, and a stability budget identifying which configuration is safe to fly. On a full-resolution corpus carrying a genuine +48 MAE aerial gap (source retrained to 14.6 validation MAE, a 34% improvement), adaptation repairs the dense-scene undercounting that would otherwise under-report a forming crush, and the flux-based risk module fires on real congestion episodes in 2 of 6 full-length clips. We localise the recoverable error: in a regime built to favor a physics-informed conservation prior (300-frame clips at 200ms spacing, five times wider than standard), the adaptation signal is normalisation-driven, not flow-driven; the continuity residual is invariant to the proportional counting errors domain shift produces, confirmed by four on/off ablations correlated at r=0.999 and a 40% input corruption moving accuracy by only 0.05 MAE. A label-free shift gate shows shift magnitude and accuracy damage are rank-independent (Spearman rho=0.20; rho=-0.60 among genuine shifts), quantifying the 58% of headroom a magnitude gate forgoes. We establish unconditional adaptation with tail monitoring as policy, closing with a six-point protocol.

</details>


### [180] [Graph Surgery and the Do-Operator: A Precise Correspondence for Acyclic Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17634)
*Satpreet Makhija*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在确定性的、有向无环的结构因果模型中，严格证明了 do-算子的两种操作（删除指向目标变量的箭头 vs 用常数替换目标机制）在依赖关系层面是等价的。核心定理表明：替换目标机制恰好删除图手术所删除的依赖关系。此外，论文还刻画了当图中存在未使用箭头时性质成立的充要条件，并讨论了干预模型、序贯干预组合以及结果只依赖实际依赖祖先的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: do-算子在因果推断中被广泛使用，其图形化表示（删除箭头）和函数化表示（替换机制为常数）被认为等价，但这种等价从未被严格地数学化表述——因为前者返回图、只记住目标，后者返回机制并保留施加的值。作者希望在依赖层面（而非语义层面）精确地比较这两种操作，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 论文在有限内生变量的确定性有向无环结构因果模型框架下，引入依赖提取算子 Graph(F) 和图形手术算子 Surg(G,T_ι)。通过数学证明推导出核心定理 Graph(F^ι)=Surg(Graph(F),T_ι)，即机制替换与图手术在依赖关系层面等价。同时刻画了当模型图 G 含未使用箭头时等价成立的充要条件，并定义了干预模型的运行方式、序贯干预的组合法则，以及依赖祖先的性质。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 核心定理证明替换目标机制恰好删除图手术所删除的依赖；(2) 对含未使用箭头的模型，刻画了等价性对每次干预都成立的充要条件——即 G 精确记录 F 的依赖关系；(3) 定义了干预模型及其运行方式，证明了序贯干预可按组合方式实现，且结果只依赖于实际依赖祖先上的干预。

Conclusion: 本文首次在依赖层面严格证明了 do-算子图形操作与函数操作的数学等价性，并给出了充分的刻画条件。这些结果为解释因果干预提供了更精确的理论基础，有助于理解为何图形化操作能够正确反映机制层面的干预效果。

Abstract: The $\operatorname{do}$-operator is described graphically by deleting arrows into its targets and functionally by replacing their mechanisms with constants. To call these operations equivalent is not yet a mathematical statement: one returns a graph and remembers only the targets, whereas the other returns mechanisms and also remembers the imposed values. We make a dependency-level comparison precise for deterministic acyclic structural causal models with finitely many endogenous variables. If $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$ extracts the dependencies of a mechanism family $F$, our main theorem is $\operatorname{Graph}(F^ι)=\operatorname{Surg}(\operatorname{Graph}(F),T_ι)$. Thus replacing target mechanisms removes exactly the dependencies removed by graph surgery. For a model $M=(G,F)$ whose graph may contain unused arrows, we characterize when the same equality holds with $G$ in place of $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$; it holds for every intervention exactly when $G$ records the dependencies of $F$ exactly. We then define the intervened model, characterize its run, show how sequential interventions combine, and prove that an outcome depends only on interventions at its actual dependency ancestors.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond the Trace: Coupling an Interpretable Reasoning-State Readout to Native MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17638)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出J64，一种从MoE推理模型的内部推理状态蒸馏出的64维语义框架，可读取模型的潜在推理过程状态；并进一步从专家路由统计重建出R64低开销代理，支持测试时决策（如分支选择、加权投票及停止重采样）和路由器编辑，显著提升推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型生成的外部文本只是其内部推理过程的局部记录，无法完整反映推理状态。作者希望揭示Mixture-of-Experts推理模型未被文本痕迹展现的潜在过程状态，从而使模型推理过程可读、可控、可部署。

Method: 1) 将词汇规模的J空间压缩为64轴语义框架J64，从模型自身的推理状态中学习；2) 从原生专家路由统计重建J64得到R64代理；3) 在已完成候选集上利用J64/R64进行单分支选择和改进加权投票；4) 在生成过程中使用滚动读取窗口实现累积停止-重采样策略；5) 基于J64命名的机制对路由器进行编辑以诱发特定推理行为。

Result: J64在推理过程中区分为推理努力与问题引致的应变；相比同rollout的token占用预测基线，held-out AUC提升0.096至0.135；R64与J64的中位每轴相关性为0.69-0.86（三个模型、两个系列），在gpt-oss-20b上保留J64预测增益的95-100%；R64加权投票在八种设置中的七种优于多数投票；J64在sibling排列对照上提升准确率1.1-5.9点，R64保留其中0.9-3.2点；路由器编辑诱导出预期推理行为，将诊断出的停滞从数值猜测转向精确符号执行。

Conclusion: J64使MoE模型的潜在推理过程状态变得可读，而R64通过原生路由统计使该状态可部署且可操作，为测试时推理控制和模型机制干预提供了新途径。

Abstract: What a reasoning model writes is only a partial record of the process that produces it. We introduce a two-level internal readout for mixture-of-experts reasoning. We first distill vocabulary-scale J-space into J64, a 64-axis semantic frame learned from the model's own reasoning states. J64 reveals readable process state that the emitted trace does not show: it separates inference effort from problem-induced strain. It also adds 0.096 to 0.135 held-out AUC over a baseline that reads the same rollout as token occupancy and aggregates it in exactly the same way. We then reconstruct J64 from native expert-routing statistics. The result is R64, a low-overhead proxy: its median per-axis correlation with J64 is 0.69 to 0.86 across three models and two families, and on gpt-oss-20b it preserves 95 to 100% of J64's predictive gain. The readout supports test-time decisions at two temporal resolutions. Over completed candidate sets, J64 and R64 improve single-branch selection, and R64-weighted voting improves plain majority voting in seven of eight settings. During generation, rolling readout windows drive a cumulative stop-and-resample policy whose operating point is fixed on training questions alone. J64 improves accuracy by 1.1 to 5.9 points over a sibling-permuted control, and the routing-only R64 proxy retains 0.9 to 3.2 of those points. Finally, router edits aimed at the mechanism J64 names induce the predicted reasoning behaviors and shift a diagnosed stall from numerical guessing toward exact symbolic execution. Together, J64 makes latent process state readable, while routing makes it deployable and actionable.

</details>


### [182] [LLM-Derived Preference Judgments Are Not Self-Consistent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17644)
*Matthew T. Ford,Francis Bahk,Jingjing Wang,Adam S. Jovine,Tinghan Ye,David B. Shmoys,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM生成的主观偏好判断（如愿意支付的价格）是否满足自身一致性（即能由单一效用函数解释），通过统计检验在六个LLM和三类商品场景中发现大量持续的不一致性，表明LLM偏好判断无法被单一效用函数忠实概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM查询偏好判断（如支付意愿）来估计效用函数并据此决策的流程正被广泛应用，但其潜在假设——判断近似自一致（可由单一效用函数复现）——从未被系统性验证。若不检验该假设，后续的效用估计与决策可能建立在不可靠的基础上。

Method: 提出统计检验和可解释的度量指标，量化观测到的偏好响应偏离最优拟合自一致效用函数的程度。在航班、公寓、酒店三类商品场景中，对六个不同的LLM进行实验测试其判断的一致性。

Result: 实验发现所有六个LLM都表现出显著且持续的不一致性：例如两个商品之间陈述的支付意愿差异，与使人在交换两者时无差异的陈述支付金额不匹配。LLM偏好判断无法被单一效用函数忠实概括。

Conclusion: LLM偏好判断存在系统性自不一致问题，依赖单一效用函数来总结LLM偏好判断的现有方法需要重新审慎评估，可能需引入更复杂的偏好模型或新的一致性校正方法。

Abstract: Agents increasingly interpret a person's natural-language preferences by querying an LLM for numerical preference judgments, e.g., by asking how much the person would be willing to pay for an item. A growing body of work estimates a utility function from these judgments and then chooses actions based on their estimated utility. This pipeline assumes the judgments are approximately self-consistent: that a single utility function can reproduce them. But are they? To study this question, we measure the self-consistency of cardinal LLM preference judgments. For example, the difference in stated willingness-to-pay between two items should match the stated payment that makes a person indifferent to exchanging them. We develop statistical tests and interpretable measures of how far observed responses depart from the best-fitting self-consistent utility function. Experiments with flight, apartment, and hotel examples across six LLMs reveal large persistent inconsistencies. This suggests that LLM-derived preference judgments cannot be faithfully summarized by a single utility function.

</details>


### [183] [GraphWake: Group Polarization via Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade in LLM-Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17665)
*Haoran Bu,Zejian Chen,Litian Zhang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的威胁模型——"记忆介导的极化级联"（Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade），利用LLM驱动代理的记忆系统作为持久化信道、公开讨论作为传播信道，通过三个阶段（暴露与记忆保持、检索与复现、迭代传播）实现群体极化操纵。作者在GraphWake框架中通过三种组件实例化该威胁，实验证明其能显著增加群体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代理可以自主在在线平台上交换观点并形成社区，这种代理运营的社交平台引入了新的安全威胁：攻击者可能操纵代理以诱导群体极化。现有方法（直接操纵代理提示词或构建回声室）在实际中难以实现，因此需要一种更现实且隐蔽的攻击范式。

Method: 提出了三个阶段的威胁模型：(1) 暴露与记忆保持阶段，攻击者向小部分目标代理暴露强化其各自立场的论证，目标代理的记忆系统处理并保留这些论证；(2) 检索与复现阶段，一个立场中立的讨论话题触发目标代理检索并复现各自保留的论证；(3) 迭代传播阶段，未处理的代理受复现论证影响后重新陈述并传播这些观点。在GraphWake框架中通过三个组件实例化：立场支持论证知识图谱构建基于知识的论证；公理化三元组选择提炼论证以实现可靠保持与复现；中立记忆线索触发并发检索与复现以启动传播。

Result: 在多个讨论场景和记忆系统上进行的实验表明，GraphWake能显著增加群体极化，揭示了社区层面的极化风险。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种新的社区级极化风险——攻击者无需直接修改代理提示或构建回音室，仅通过利用代理的记忆机制和公开讨论的自然传播即可有效放大群体极化，为代理社交媒体平台的安全防护提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents can autonomously exchange opinions on online platforms and form communities. Such agent-operated social platforms raise a new security concern: attackers may manipulate agents to induce group polarization. Existing methods manipulate agent prompts or construct echo chambers, both of which are difficult to realize in practice. We therefore formulate a new threat, Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade, which uses agent memory as a persistence channel and public discussion as a propagation channel. This threat contains three stages. During exposure and memory retention, the attacker exposes a small set of target agents to arguments that reinforce their respective stated stances. The targets' memory systems then process and retain these arguments. During retrieval and reproduction, a shared stance-neutral discussion cues the targets to retrieve and reproduce their respective retained arguments. During iterative propagation, untreated agents influenced by the reproduced arguments restate and spread them. We instantiate this threat in GraphWake with three components: (i) stance-support argumentation knowledge graphs construct knowledge-based arguments; (ii) axiom-oriented triple selection distills them for reliable retention and reproduction; and (iii) stance-neutral memory cueing triggers concurrent retrieval and reproduction, initiating propagation. Experiments across multiple discussions and memory systems show that GraphWake substantially increases group polarization. These findings reveal a community-level polarization risk.

</details>


### [184] [Auditing Self-Evolution in Financial Agents: Capability Gains, Security Drift, and Execution-Interface Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17684)
*Jialong Li,Jialing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了三种自进化智能体（SkillOpt、AWM、ReasoningBank）在模拟电子银行场景中的安全性与可靠性，发现仅追踪准确率会掩盖能力退化、攻击暴露增加和未经授权的金融状态变更等风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体能够将经验转化为可复用的技能、工作流或记忆，但现有的评估仅关注进化后的准确率，无法揭示学习行为是否破坏了先前正确的行为或引发了安全漏洞。金融场景下需要更全面的审计框架。

Method: 在模拟电子银行环境中，使用匹配的良性采集轨迹、密封评估端点、执行接地检查和独立状态重放，对SkillOpt、Agent Workflow Memory (AWM) 和 ReasoningBank 三种方法进行系统审计。采用Qwen 3.7 Flash作为基础模型，并进行了三种独立进化谱系的对比实验及事后敏感性测试。

Result: SkillOpt提升良性效用（0.741→0.837），但注入内容暴露上升（0.820→0.943），条件攻击成功率下降（0.605→0.562）而总体ASR上升（0.496→0.530），未经授权的金融状态变更升至0.685。ReasoningBank提升效用至0.859且不增加总ASR，但未授权状态变更仍略高于基线。AWM的WebArena文本-动作封装破坏了原生函数调用执行器的工具执行，移除该封装后效用从0.319恢复至0.756，但暴露升至0.909、ASR升至0.575。

Conclusion: 审计自进化金融智能体不能仅看准确率，必须同时跟踪能力回归、攻击面接触、未经授权的金融状态变更以及工件-执行器兼容性四个维度。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents turn experience into reusable skills, workflows, or memories, but post-evolution accuracy alone does not show whether learned behavior preserves previously correct behavior or security. We audit SkillOpt, Agent Workflow Memory (AWM), and ReasoningBank in simulated e-banking using matched benign acquisition trajectories, sealed evaluation endpoints, execution-grounded checks, and independent state replay. On Qwen 3.7 Flash, SkillOpt raises benign utility from 0.741 to 0.837 while exposure to injected content rises from 0.820 to 0.943. Conditional attack success after exposure falls from 0.605 to 0.562, yet overall attack success rate (ASR) rises from 0.496 to 0.530 and unauthorized financial state changes rise to 0.685. Across three independently evolved lineages, capability, exposure, and unauthorized-state changes increase in all three, whereas ASR increases in only two. ReasoningBank raises utility to 0.859 without increasing aggregate ASR, although unauthorized state changes remain slightly above Static. AWM reveals a separate evaluation hazard: a literal WebArena text-action envelope disrupts tool execution in our native function-calling executor. In a post-hoc sensitivity test, removing only that envelope restores utility from 0.319 to 0.756, while exposure rises from 0.299 to 0.909 and ASR from 0.195 to 0.575. Auditing self-evolving financial agents therefore requires tracking regressions, attack-surface contact, unauthorized financial-state change, and artifact-executor compatibility, not accuracy alone.

</details>


### [185] [Mixture-of-Expert Blocks Contain Strong Hallucination Detection Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17687)
*Joao Fonseca,Rodrigo Rodrigues,Paolo Romano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InnerExpert，首个利用混合专家（MoE）架构内部信号进行逐token幻觉检测的方法。通过结合路由级信号（如路由器熵、专家分歧、专家使用模式）与标准Transformer信号，构造紧凑的逐token特征向量，由轻量级检测器分类，在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在幻觉问题（生成看似合理但虚假的内容），现有检测方法多在答案或句子级别操作，缺乏逐token级别的检测能力，难以精确定位幻觉片段并实现细粒度干预。MoE架构在单次前向传播中产生独特的内部信号（路由器熵、专家分歧、专家使用模式），这些信号在稠密架构中不可用且此前未被用于幻觉检测。

Method: InnerExpert方法包括：(1) 从MoE架构中提取路由级信号（路由器熵、专家分歧、专家使用模式）以及标准Transformer信号；(2) 将两类信号组合为紧凑的逐token特征向量；(3) 使用轻量级检测器对这些特征进行分类；(4) 通过LLM-as-a-judge流水线生成训练标签，支持无需人工标注的持续模型更新。整个过程仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上，InnerExpert优于现有方法，达到最高0.91的答案级AUROC和0.76的token级AUROC，且仅需单次前向传播。

Conclusion: MoE特有的路由内部信号对逐token幻觉检测具有重要价值，InnerExpert验证了这一方向的有效性，为幻觉定位和细粒度干预提供了可行方案，同时LLM-as-a-judge标签流水线使得方法可持续更新且无需人工标注。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited by a fundamental problem: the generation of plausible but false content, known as hallucinations. Most existing detection methods operate at the answer or sentence level, yet per-token detection is essential for localizing hallucinated spans and enabling fine-grained interventions. In this paper, we explore the use of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm to address this gap. In MoE architectures, a single forward pass activates a sparse subset of experts (i.e., distinct feedforward networks per layer) via a routing mechanism, producing internal signals (e.g., router entropy, expert disagreement, and expert usage patterns) that are unavailable in dense architectures and have not been previously exploited for hallucination detection. To this end, we introduce InnerExpert, the first method to leverage these MoE-specific signals for per-token hallucination detection. InnerExpert combines routing-level and standard transformer signals into compact per-token feature vectors, classified by a lightweight detector trained on labels produced by an LLM-as-a-judge pipeline, which enables continuous model updates without manual annotation. Our results show that InnerExpert outperforms existing methods across five datasets and two MoE architectures, achieving up to 0.91 answer-level and 0.76 token-level AUROC, while requiring only a single forward pass.

</details>


### [186] [Accuracy and Robustness of Model Cascades Under Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17711)
*Pallavi Mitra,Jai Kushwaha,Felix Biessmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了置信度级联模型框架在图像分类任务中，面对静态损坏和顺序扰动等输入退化时路由决策的变化，识别出三种失效模式，并指出能效级联模型需要在干净准确率之外评估路由可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测级联通过将简单输入路由到轻量模型、困难样本交由大型模型处理来显著降低AI模型的能耗，但其有效性依赖于基于置信度的路由可靠性。输入退化（如静态损坏和顺序扰动）可能改变模型置信度和路由决策，而现有评估往往只关注干净数据上的准确率，忽略了分布偏移下路由行为的退化风险。

Method: 作者在准确率、路由质量和能耗的帕累托最优点上选择一个模型级联（相比基线可实现高达10倍的CO₂排放降低），然后系统地研究该级联在输入损坏（静态损坏和顺序扰动）下的行为，分析输入分布偏移时路由决策的变化。

Result: 分析识别出三种失效模式：(1) 静态损坏破坏路由信号但大型模型仍有用；(2) 静态损坏同时退化两个模型，使延迟路由不再能恢复准确率；(3) 顺序扰动使预测稳定但路由抑制增强，产生稳定但不可靠的预测。

Conclusion: 能效模型级联需要超越干净准确率的评估方法，必须明确关注分布偏移下的路由可靠性，否则其节能优势可能以预测可靠性为代价。

Abstract: Prediction cascades significantly reduce energy consumption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models while maintaining high predictive performance. The idea is that easy inputs are routed through a lightweight small model, and difficult uncertain cases are deferred to a larger model. While this design can improve computational efficiency on clean data, its effectiveness depends on the reliability of confidence-based routing. Input degradations, such as static corruptions and sequential perturbations, can shift model confidence and routing decisions. In this paper, we study confidence-based cascade frameworks for image classification and investigate how such degradations affect their confidence-based deferral behavior. We select a model cascade at the pareto-optimum of accuracy, routing quality, and energy consumption that achieves competitive predictive performance with an up to 10-fold decrease in CO$_2$ emissions. We study the behavior of that model cascade under input corruptions and analyze how the cascade's routing decisions change when the input distribution shifts. Our analysis identifies three failure modes. Static corruptions either (1) break the routing signal while the large model remains useful, or (2) degrade both models so deferral no longer recovers accuracy. Sequential perturbations reveal a third mode: predictions stabilize but deferral suppresses, yielding stable but unreliable predictions. These findings demonstrate that energy efficient model cascades require evaluation beyond clean accuracy, with explicit attention to routing reliability under distribution shift.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Suspicious Steps: Ontological Trust in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17718)
*An He,Yao Wang,Haibin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出本体论信任（ontological trust）概念，将其实例化为RGE在线监控器，将轨迹前缀的信任分解为角色（Role）、目标（Goal）和证据（Evidence）三个维度，用于检测长时程智能体在工具调用过程中的前缀级漂移，而非仅局部动作合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在多个步骤、工具和观察中运行时，仅检查每个动作是否局部合法是不够的——轨迹前缀可能逐渐偏离用户授权的任务（如角色扩大、目标偏移或使用未经用户提供的证据），而现有监控器要么检查局部合规、要么给出整条轨迹的最终判定、要么只做通用风险打分，无法直接估计这种前缀级别的任务关系。

Method: 提出RGE在线监控器：利用LLM仅生成结构化的任务与步骤表示，信任状态更新、投影和干预决策均为确定性计算。将信任沿角色（Role）、目标（Goal）和证据（Evidence）三个维度分解，输出可回放、可审计的信任轨迹而非单一端到端判定。在OSWorld、FinanceBench和EICU-AC构建的跨领域轨迹语料上，覆盖良性执行、前缀配对漂移和伪一致性失败三种类型。

Result: 在跨领域轨迹语料上，RGE在前缀配对漂移检测上优于规则式、判定式和护盾式基线；使用两个更大的估计器模型时，在每个基准上Drift F1超过93%，同时良性覆盖率保持在95.8%以上。伪一致性检测难度更高，其检测效果取决于任务完成是否外部可见，这是一个被经验表征的结构性限制。

Conclusion: RGE通过确定性的信任状态更新实现可审计的在线监控，有效检测长时程智能体的前缀级漂移；同时揭示了一个结构性限制：当任务完成不具有外部可见性时，伪一致性失败难以被可靠检测。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly operate across many steps, tools, and observa- tions. In this setting, the relevant oversight question is not only whether each action is locally valid, but whether the evolving trajectory still corresponds to the task the user authorized. Drift can accumulate quietly: an agent may call the right tool with plausible arguments at every step, while its prefix moves toward a broader role, an adjacent objective, or evidence the user never supplied. Existing monitors mostly check local compliance, deliver final-trace verdicts, or score generic risk; they do not directly estimate this prefix-level relation. We introduce ontological trust, a task-conditioned property of trajectory prefixes, and instantiate it as RGE, an online monitor that decomposes trust along Role, Goal, and Evidence. RGE uses LLMs only to derive structured task and step representations; trust-state updates, projec- tions, and intervention decisions are deterministic, so the output is a replayable and auditable trust trajectory rather than a single end-to-end judge verdict. We construct a cross-domain trajectory corpus from OSWorld, FinanceBench, and EICU-AC, covering benign executions, prefix-paired drift, and pseudo-consistency failures. On this corpus, RGE outperforms adapted rule-, judge-, and shield-style baselines on prefix-paired drift detection. With the two larger estimator models, it exceeds 93% Drift F1 on every benchmark while keeping benign coverage at or above 95.8%. Pseudo-consistency is harder: detection depends on whether task completion is externally visible, a structural limit we characterize empirically.

</details>


### [188] [Grounding Healthcare LLMs in a Causal Knowledge Graph: Framework, Metrics, and a Cardiovascular Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15382)
*Ummara Mumtaz,Aimen Noor,Awais Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的、以图为中心的评估框架，用于评估大语言模型在医疗保健中面向干预的行为。该框架包含领域因果知识图谱、场景条件子图提取、四种受控的接地条件以及基于断言语义标识符的自动化评分流水线。在心血管数据集上测试发现，集成条件C4在因果边F1、不良效应F1、证据准确率和无根据声明率等指标上最强，而未经接地的C1虽原始干预准确率最高，但缺乏因果与证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在医疗决策支持中的评估仍以单答案准确率为导向，忽略了干预、机制、危害、证据和不确定性等关键推理维度。为了更全面地评估LLM在医疗场景中的行为，需要一种可复现、结构化且能区分不同推理能力的评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件框架：(i) 领域因果知识图谱，断言作为带稳定标识符和来源追溯的一等节点；(ii) 场景条件的子图提取步骤，根据临床场景检索相关断言子图；(iii) 四种受控接地条件（C1无接地、C2知识图谱、C3因果图、C4集成），变化子图在模型上下文中的组成方式；(iv) 基于断言标识符的自动化评分管线，单次计算干预准确性等多项指标。使用跨八种推理失败模式的类别平衡场景生成器在心血管因果图上进行压力测试。

Result: C4集成条件取得最强因果边F1（0.838）、不良效应F1（0.833）、证据准确率（0.738）和最低无根据声明率（0.114）；C1无接地取得最高原始干预准确率（0.948）但无可测量的因果或证据接地。指标面板在不同条件下沿可解释且非冗余的轴进行区分。

Conclusion: 该框架能够系统地评估LLM在医疗保健中面向干预的推理行为，揭示了接地条件在因果与证据推理与原始准确性之间的权衡，表明图接地结构化的知识对提升LLM的循证推理至关重要，同时仅靠高干预准确率不足以证明可靠的医疗决策支持能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for healthcare decision support, but their evaluations still reward single-answer accuracy rather than reasoning about interventions, mechanisms, harms, evidence, and uncertainty. We propose a reproducible, graph-centered evaluation framework for intervention-oriented LLM behavior in healthcare and stress-test it in a cardiovascular pilot. The framework has four components: (i) a domain causal knowledge graph in which assertions are first-class, provenance-preserving nodes with stable identifiers; (ii) a scenario-conditioned subgraph extraction step that, given any clinical scenario, retrieves the relevant reified-assertion subgraph; (iii) four controlled grounding conditions that vary how the retrieved subgraph is composed into the model's context (ungrounded C1, knowledge-graph C2, causal-graph C3, integrated C4); and (iv) an automated scoring pipeline, anchored on assertion identifiers, that computes intervention accuracy, and other evaluation measures on a single pass. To test the framework, we built a category-balanced scenario generator across eight reasoning failure modes and instantiated it on a cardiovascular graph. The metric panel discriminates conditions along interpretable, non-redundant axes: C4 obtains the strongest causal edge F1 (0.838), adverse-effect F1 (0.833), evidence accuracy (0.738), and unsupported claim rate (0.114), while C1 obtains the highest raw intervention accuracy (0.948) with no measurable causal or evidential grounding.

</details>


### [189] [Evaluating the Diversity of AI-Generated Content with Diversity Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17731)
*Xiuyuan Hu,Xuege Hou,Guoqing Liu,Yang Zhao,Jieran Li,Dongbiao Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Hao Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI多样性评估被简化为单一标量分数本质上是欠定义的（under-specified），并通过公理化分析和实证分析诊断了现有标量多样性指标的局限性，提出"多样性轮廓"（diversity profiles）——一种基于曲线的、条件感知的评估框架，在指定表示和距离/核函数下跨阈值、尺度、指数或阶数评估参数化多样性族，以提供更透明、分辨率感知的多样性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性是评估生成式AI系统的关键标准，但现有方法将多样性压缩为单一标量分数，这些标量可能编码不同的归纳偏置，且对同一组样本可能给出相互矛盾的排名。作者认为多样性评估在简化为单一数字时本质上是不充分的，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 论文的方法包括三个部分：(1) 综述代表性多样性指标；(2) 从两个互补视角诊断其局限性——公理化分析证明没有任何代表性标量指标能同时满足所有期望属性，实证分析表明高维表示空间会产生集中且模态相关的距离分布；(3) 提出多样性轮廓（diversity profiles），即曲线值、条件感知的摘要，在指定表示和距离/核函数下跨阈值、尺度、指数或阶数参数化评估多样性族，并实例化多种代表性指标族的轮廓。

Result: 论文证明现有标量多样性指标在公理上无法同时满足所有理想属性，并在实证上揭示高维空间中距离分布集中导致的问题；提出的多样性轮廓能够揭示比较结果是否在分辨率上稳健或依赖于任意参数选择，并在多个代表性指标族中实例化，展示了其在生成式AI评估中的实际用途。

Conclusion: 多样性轮廓为比较生成式AI内容的多样性提供了更透明和分辨率感知的框架，克服了单一标量分数在多样性评估中的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Diversity is a fundamental criterion for evaluating generative artificial intelligence (AI) systems, yet its measurement remains inherently ambiguous. Existing approaches typically represent generated samples in an embedding space, compute pairwise distances or similarities, and aggregate them into a single scalar score. Such scalar summaries are convenient, but they often encode different inductive biases and may yield contradictory rankings of the same sample sets. In this paper, we argue that diversity evaluation for AI-generated content is intrinsically under-specified when reduced to a single number. We first review representative diversity metrics, and then diagnose their limitations from two complementary perspectives: an axiomatic analysis showing that no representative scalar metric satisfies all desirable properties simultaneously, and an empirical analysis showing that high-dimensional representation spaces can induce concentrated, modality-dependent distance distributions. To address these issues, we propose diversity profiles: curve-valued, condition-aware summaries that evaluate a parameterized diversity family across a range of thresholds, scales, exponents, or orders under a specified representation and distance or kernel function. Diversity profiles reveal whether a comparison is robust across resolutions or instead depends on an arbitrary parameter choice. We instantiate profiles for several representative metric families and demonstrate their practical use in generative AI evaluation. Overall, diversity profiles provide a more transparent and resolution-aware framework for comparing the diversity of AI-generated content.

</details>


### [190] [Neuro-symbolic learning over OWL 2 DL via consequence-based compilation to differentiable circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17741)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Baobab系统，将非Horn描述逻辑SROIQ（OWL 2 DL）本体编译为Sentential Decision Diagram（SDD），通过证据条件加权模型计数训练感知网络，并针对推理捷径问题提出基于justification索引的混合模型，首次在非Horn描述逻辑中刻画并缓解推理捷径，编译器和表示结果均经Lean 4机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号（NeSy）学习器在描述逻辑上要么将本体嵌入连续空间而放弃经典蕴涵，要么限制在Horn片段EL++（只有单一典范模型）。这无法处理OWL 2 DL（SROIQ）这样丰富的本体，且现有方法缺乏对推理捷径（reasoning shortcuts）问题的刻画与缓解。

Method: Baobab将SROIQ本体（含有限ABox）编译为SDD：先基于后果演算（consequence-based calculus）饱和命题核心，再在活跃域（active domain）上实例化剩余的SROIQ特征（nominal、基数约束、角色公理）。随后利用SDD的证据条件加权模型计数训练感知网络，并以查询的justification为索引构造混合模型来校准后验，从电路枚举的补全中初始化。

Result: 在一个涵盖所有独特SROIQ特征的本体上，CNN能够学习读取由后继关系耦合的MNIST数字，并恢复独立感知器仅为随机水平的潜在本体概念。当监督允许多个本体一致的补全时，独立感知会塌缩到单一补全（推理捷径）；以justification为索引的混合模型在真实图像MNIST任务上达到贝叶斯最优后验，而单次WMC和BEARS-ensemble假设类的学习混合均无法做到。

Conclusion: Baobab是首个在非Horn描述逻辑（SROIQ）中刻画并缓解推理捷径的工作，编译器健全性和表示结果均在Lean 4中机器验证，提供了开源代码。

Abstract: OWL 2 DL ontologies, grounded in the description logic $\mathcal{SROIQ}$, express large knowledge bases in biomedicine and the Semantic Web. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learners over description logics either embed the ontology in a continuous space, abandoning classical entailment, or restrict to the Horn fragment $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$, which has a single canonical model. We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [191] [The Curious Case of Exploding DecPOMDPs: Containing the Fire through Policy Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17749)
*Nazlı Nur Karabulut,tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模多智能体部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（DecPOMDPs）中智能体数量带来的指数复杂性难题，提出将关注点从"计数智能体"转向"计数策略"，并据此定义策略计数DecPOMDP，同时设计了基于紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划算法来高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DecPOMDPs是建模多智能体不确定性决策的通用框架，但其复杂度随智能体数量呈指数增长。虽然通过对具有对称性的智能体进行分区并按计数（counting）进行紧凑编码可以降低模型复杂度和评估成本至多项式依赖，但策略空间仍然会爆炸式增长，这一问题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 本文提出将研究重点从计数智能体转向计数策略，从而为所谓的策略计数DecPOMDP（policy-counted DecPOMDPs）在智能体数量上实现可解性。在此基础上，利用紧凑表示设计了策略计数动态规划（policy-counted dynamic programming）算法来高效求解该类问题。

Result: 通过计数策略而非计数智能体，策略计数DecPOMDP在智能体数量维度上实现了可解性（tractability），并配合所提出的策略计数动态规划算法，利用紧凑表示高效求解问题。

Conclusion: 策略计数方法为大规模DecPOMDP的求解开辟了新思路，通过统计策略而非智能体来规避指数复杂性，结合紧凑表示和动态规划方法可实现对策略计数DecPOMDP的高效求解。

Abstract: Decentralised partially observable Markov decision processes (DecPOMDPs) provide a general framework for modelling multi-agent decision making under uncertainty. However, DecPOMDPs are known to suffer from exponential complexity in the number of agents. One way to combat this intractability in agent numbers is to look at partitions of agents that exhibit a form of symmetry among agents, allowing for a compact encoding by counting. However, a challenge arises as the policy space explodes, even though the model complexity and evaluation cost reduce to a polynomial dependence. In this paper, we redirect our focus from counting agents to counting policies, which actually enables tractability in agent numbers for so called policy-counted DecPOMDPs. Further, we present policy-counted dynamic programming using the compact representation to solve policy-counted DecPOMDPs efficiently.

</details>


### [192] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出D²ACCI协议——一个双循环的闭环迭代协议，用于LLM代理持久记忆系统的诊断驱动开发。它通过外部诊断门控、配对统计比较、保护切片监控和溯源级可定位性来支持记忆干预的推广、功能标志或拒绝。作者还引入DCR分级可观测性指标和D²ACCI-Eval可复用评测工件，并在MemStack实例上于三个公开基准上取得优异性能（LoCoMo 93.59%、LongMemEval 90.93%、PersonaMem-V2 57.20%），五项配对消融证明若干增强带来显著增益，且诊断追踪的DCR达到98-100%而结果日志为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的持久记忆系统采用多阶段流水线（摄取、检索、过滤、生成），失败难以定位到具体阶段。现有评估通常仅报告聚合性能，缺乏配对统计比较、受保护切片非回归检查或阶段级诊断追踪，导致记忆干预的迭代缺乏可靠证据基础。

Method: 提出双循环协议D²ACCI：外部诊断门控基于配对证据、受保护切片监控和溯源可定位性来推广/功能标志/拒绝记忆干预；引入DCR分级可观测性指标衡量失败可定位性；构建D²ACCI-Eval可复用评测工件支持门控重放；在MemStack系统中实例化协议。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-V2上分别达到93.59%、90.93%、57.20%。五项配对消融中，补充提取、会话记忆检索和Forget Guard带来+1.9至+3.7pp的显著增益（p≤.003）。BM25/RRF被保留为受监控的功能标志。诊断追踪的DCR@3达到98-100%，而结果日志为0%。

Conclusion: 稳健的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计根基扎实且回归感知的证据，这正是D²ACCI所填补的空白。该方法证明了从仅聚合评估转向诊断驱动评估的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [193] [StartupBench: Benchmarking General-Purpose Agents on Market-Validated End-to-End Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17800)
*Liya Zhu,Xin Ma,Tao Liu,Haodong Wang,Ge Zhang,Jingzhe Ding,Qingshui Gu,Yongjie Zhong,Jinxiang Meng,Yuan Gao,Yunqiu Zhou,Hao Zhu,Jifeng He,Yongzhi Liao,Xinyi Zhang,Chaoxin Li,Yi Zhu,Xi Lin,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Wen Zhang,Yunyang Wang,Duo Wang,Huan Zhou,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Kaiyuan Zhang,Chuqian Yu,Tianhao Yu,Longxiang Liu,Jianbo Xue,Huimin Che,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StartupBench，一个基于市场验证的AI初创产品工作流构建的端到端智能体基准测试。在最强的模型上也仅有约30%的任务被成功完成，揭示当前通用智能体在真实用户工作流上的能力仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多依赖研究者预先选择的任务，无法代表真实用户对AI系统的实际需求，因此无法准确衡量LLM和智能体能力提升是否真正惠及现实世界的工作场景。

Method: 系统性地研究具有市场验证采用度的AI初创产品及其产品工作流和用户，从中识别跨专业领域具有实际需求的任务，将工作流转化为完整的交付物导向任务，并使用细粒度评分标准（rubrics）进行评估。

Result: 在统一的智能体评测框架下，即使最强的模型也只能完成约30%的任务，尽管在许多任务上取得了实质性部分进展。进一步分析发现复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识是主要的失败来源。

Conclusion: 许多市场验证的工作流仍超出当前通用智能体的可靠能力范围，StartupBench可作为衡量端到端真实用户任务完成进展的实证基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models(LLMs) and agents have substantially improved the ability of AI systems to execute complex tasks. Yet existing benchmarks largely rely on researcher-selected tasks, leaving uncertain whether such progress extends to the work that real-world users actually demand from AI systems. We introduce \textbf{StartupBench}, an E2E agent benchmark grounded in market-validated AI startup products. Rather than defining tasks from pre-defined assumptions about useful agent capabilities, we systematically study AI products with demonstrated adoption, together with their product workflows and users, to identify real-world tasks for which AI has established practical demand across diverse professional domains. We translate these workflows into complete deliverable-oriented tasks and evaluate them with fine-grained rubrics capturing their complex requirements. Across representative models evaluated under a unified agent harness, even the strongest model successfully completes only approximately 30\% of StartupBench, despite making substantial partial progress on many tasks. Further analysis identifies aspects like complex instruction following and domain-specific expertise as major sources of failure. Our results reveal that many market-validated workflows remain beyond the reliable capabilities of current general-purpose agents, establishing StartupBench as an empirical measure of progress toward E2E completions of real-world user tasks.

</details>


### [194] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARASH方法，通过局部邻域分析自适应地选择最相关的样本（shots）来优化表格基础模型（如TabPFN）的上下文学习效率，大幅减少提示长度和内存占用，同时保持相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测是众多应用中的关键任务。虽然专门的表格基础模型（TFMs）如TabPFN表现出色，但它们需要大量计算资源且频繁重训练不可行。上下文学习（ICL）中的少样本提示提供了一种资源高效的替代方案，但如何为表格数据选择最相关的样本行以作为提示仍然是一个挑战。

Method: ARASH（Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection）方法，通过训练集内的局部邻域分析来为每个查询自适应地选择和检索最优的样本（shots），以提升TFM的效率。

Result: ARASH将TabPFN的提示长度和内存使用分别减少了1261.5倍和2.56倍，同时保持了可比的准确率。

Conclusion: ARASH通过查询自适应的样本选择策略，在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了表格基础模型的资源效率，为表格领域的大规模部署提供了实用方案。
