Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [195] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch，一个两阶段自主研究系统，将Idea Generation（想法生成）与Idea Execution（想法执行）相连接，通过多模型生成与交叉评审产生可验证的研究计划，并通过协调的agent代理将计划拆解为实验、迭代实施与诊断，最终通过独立基于证据的评审才接受研究结论，实现"Insight In, Hallucination Out"的可信研究流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统已能执行长期研究流程，但自动化本身并不能保证研究过程在科学上是严谨可靠的——即自动化可能导致产生不可靠或幻觉性的实验结果，缺乏科学基础的保障。

Method: AutoResearch包含两个阶段：1) Idea Generation阶段，持续整合新兴研究信号与累积领域知识，识别可迁移的机制性洞见，通过多模型生成和交叉评审产出有根据且可测试的研究计划；2) Idea Execution阶段，协调agent将计划分解为实验、迭代实现并诊断问题，采用独立基于证据的评审后才接受研究结论。

Result: 在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动的机器学习等代表性场景中，AutoResearch能将生成的想法转化为可衡量的进展，检测并纠正不可靠的实验结果，并进行基于证据的决策（继续、修订或终止研究方向）。例如在RSICD基准上，生成的idea将平均Recall从32.84提升至34.69，且仅记录5个审计确认的问题事件，而其他自主研究系统为11-27个。

Conclusion: AutoResearch展示了一种研究流程：在实验前对有意义洞见进行扎根验证，在接受结论前对实验结果进行扎根验证——即"输入洞见，输出幻觉消除"（Insight In, Hallucination Out），证明了通过两阶段设计可以有效提升自主研究系统的科学严谨性。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [196] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的自适应策略组合问题：离线合成有限数量的无记忆随机策略，并在部署后用轻量级在线选择器在不同环境中挑选最优策略，以缓解传统RMDP的保守性。作者给出了策略组合认证和合成的复杂度理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）针对一组可能的转移函数优化单一策略，但这种方式在真实动力学固定且部署后可部分识别的情况下过于保守。为此需要一种既能离线合成灵活策略集、又能在线轻量适配的方案。

Method: 提出自适应策略组合（adaptive policy portfolios）框架：离线合成有限的无记忆随机策略集合，配以轻量级在线选择器；以鲁棒遗憾（robust regret）作为组合质量的度量指标，并从复杂度理论角度分析组合认证与合成的计算难度。

Result: 复杂度结果如下：(1) 在无环(s,a)-矩形RMDP中，确定性组合的认证问题是∀ℝ-完全的；(2) 在一般有理多胞形上，即使带固定折扣和无环动力学，合成一元有界大小的组合是∃∀ℝ-完全的；(3) 单策略情形在组合和代数层面均已困难。此外提出了一个可运行时特化的离线组合构造方法。

Conclusion: 本工作首次从复杂度理论角度刻画了RMDP自适应策略组合的困难性，表明组合认证和合成即使在简单设定下也具有高复杂度（涉及实数存在/全称量词层次），为进一步设计可处理的近似算法或启发式提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [197] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了基于记忆的自我改进智能体，发现其存在两个脆弱性问题：评估噪声大且高度依赖任务顺序。作者通过添加更详细的规范信息（如rubrics和环境反馈）来缓解问题，但差距仍然存在，呼吁更严格的评估协议和有效的人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型自我改进智能体在近期文献中展现出巨大潜力，但其可靠性方面被严重忽视。作者希望重新审视这些方法的稳定性，揭示其在多次运行和任务顺序变化下的真实表现。

Method: 对两种记忆型方法进行全面重新评估，扩展两个维度：(1) 多次运行以量化方差；(2) 随机打乱任务顺序以研究顺序效应。同时通过人工检查智能体记忆形成假设，并通过引入详细rubrics和环境反馈来验证任务/环境欠规范化的假设。

Result: 发现两个关键问题：一是智能体评估在复杂环境和多步任务中本质上有噪声，自我改进循环会放大噪声；二是改进效果高度依赖任务顺序，默认顺序形成了隐式课程。添加更详细规范信息仅部分弥合了性能差距，仍有未知因素导致脆弱性。

Conclusion: 呼吁采用更严格的评估协议（多次运行报告、压力测试）来评估自我改进智能体，并倡导构建支持有效人类监督的系统，防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


### [198] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoTS-Agent，一个基于验证反馈引导的自进化LLM智能体，用于金融时间序列变点检测。通过三种互补算子（Revision、Alternative Strategy、Recombination）演化可执行的实验轨迹，在四个基准数据集上超越现有基于LLM的智能体，并保持100%的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳和异质的统计特性，不存在单一的无监督算法能在不同资产和市场体制下稳定表现。传统工作流高度依赖专家人工进行模型选择、特征设计和超参数调优，缺乏可扩展性和适应性。因此需要一个能自主探索并适应不同数据集统计特性的检测方案。

Method: EvoTS-Agent首先进行策划性的探索性数据分析（EDA）以刻画数据集特性并初始化候选检测模型；随后通过三种互补算子演化可执行的实验轨迹：Revision（利用当前最优解）、Alternative Strategy（在进展停滞时探索根本不同的建模方向）、Recombination（综合高性能轨迹中的互补证据）。验证反馈在整个搜索过程中引导轨迹演化，使检测流水线自适应每个数据集的统计特性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，EvoTS-Agent在所有评估的后端LLM上均持续优于现有基于LLM的智能体，同时保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 验证反馈引导的自进化机制能够使LLM智能体有效适应金融时间序列的异质统计特性，在兼顾可靠执行的前提下实现自主化的变点检测流水线构建。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [199] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在程序化内容生成中，通过大语言模型变异和交叉操作直接演化Python生成器，并提出持续抽象发现（CAD）方法提取可复用原语到辅助模块中，实验表明CAD在Sokoban、Zelda、Dangerous Dave和Lode Runner四个领域均能提升演化搜索的最终适应度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成可执行程序，这使得可以直接在程序化内容生成器上进行搜索而非逐关搜索。然而，从零搜索生成器缺乏可复用的组件积累，导致搜索效率受限。本文旨在探索如何从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语，以提升演化程序搜索的效果。

Method: 提出持续抽象发现（Continual Abstraction Discovery, CAD）方法：在演化过程中，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到运行专属的辅助模块中。采用2×2实验设计，交叉比对是否使用CAD与是否提供固定手写领域API两种情况。每个演化运行通过语言模型变异和交叉操作进化完整的Python生成器。

Result: 数据集包含160次完整演化运行，每个实验单元格至少有10次50代运行。CAD在所有八个领域与API组合比较中均提升了平均最终最佳适应度。在演化过程中，学习到的库被大多数后续程序采用，并反复发现了验证、可达性和结构性工具函数。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在演化程序搜索中发现可复用原语能够提升内容生成器的搜索效果，支持了基于语言模型的演化程序搜索结合抽象发现范式在程序化内容生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [200] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号世界模型（neurosymbolic world model），其中奖励预测仅依赖于潜状态中结构化的符号组件子集，通过解耦观测重建与奖励预测，使世界模型能够零样本泛化到定义在相同符号状态空间上的新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优的基于模型强化学习方法学习的神经世界模型虽然表达能力强，但潜表示不可解释且与训练任务绑定，难以泛化到新任务。作者希望通过引入结构化符号组件来提升世界模型的可解释性和任务泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种神经符号世界模型框架，将奖励预测解耦，使其仅依赖于潜状态中结构化的符号子集，而观测重建依赖完整潜状态。这种解耦设计使世界模型能零样本适应定义在同一符号状态空间上的新奖励函数。

Result: 作者讨论了该方法的主要优势与挑战，并通过实验证明神经符号世界模型在泛化性能上显著优于纯神经方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号世界模型通过结构化符号状态组件的引入，实现了零样本的任务泛化，克服了纯神经世界模型任务依赖性强、泛化能力弱的局限。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [201] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FlightLLM，一种基于先验引导的语义大语言模型方法，用于可解释的飞行安全分析，通过特征工程、语义离散化、CatBoost先验注入、对比少样本学习和结构化提示词，在704个A320真实飞行样本上实现了竞争力的分类性能并生成了直接合理的成因解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI技术（如特征重要性图）需要大量领域知识才能转化为具操作意义的解释。LLM虽擅长语言推理，但在飞行安全领域面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、任务数据稀缺、缺乏领域知识等关键挑战。

Method: FlightLLM框架包含五个核心组件：(1)特征工程，结合统计描述符与物理飞行指标以解决模态不一致；(2)语义离散化模块，将抽象数值模式转换为定性描述；(3)CatBoost作为统计专家注入先验预测到提示词中；(4)对比少样本学习策略弥补数据稀缺；(5)结构化提示词嵌入航空领域知识。

Result: 在704个A320真实飞行样本（以硬着陆为锚点事件）上评估，FlightLLM在保持竞争力的分类性能的同时，能够生成直接且合理的飞行事件成因解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM有效解决了LLM在飞行安全分析中面临的多重挑战，实现了分类性能与可解释性的平衡，为基于LLM的可解释飞行安全分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [202] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StagedWorkspace，一个面向知识工作智能体的版本化工作区，将解析视图和评审diff显式绑定到原生文件的哈希版本，解决了AI智能体在搜索、编辑、评审、提交过程中因引用不同版本工件而导致的状态不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地执行知识工作（生成和修改代码仓库、文档、表格、幻灯片等持久化数字工件），但它们在搜索时解析的视图、编辑的原生文件、评审的变更以及提交的工件可能引用同一工作产品的不同版本。编程智能体已通过仓库契约（search、diffs、tests）部分解决此问题，而PDF、表格、幻灯片、笔记本及混合格式项目文件夹中缺乏类似的显式契约，导致工作区状态不一致。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace：一个版本化工作区，将解析记录和评审diff绑定到原生文件内容哈希上，随文件变化动态更新。通过与固定基准（OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents）上的消融实验对比单一视图（仅解析或仅原生）与双重访问（解析+原生）的性能差异，同时进行评审轴消融实验验证diff可见性的影响。

Result: 在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents基准上，双重解析/原生访问在所有测试模型上取得最高点估计值；相比单一视图，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1分，APEX平均rubric得分提升4.7-9.2分。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上以Gemini 3.1 Pro取得63.9%，在APEX上以GPT-5.4 Nano取得42.1分，对比同模型发布成绩29.3%和25.5分有显著提升。在57个文件编辑任务的配对评审轴消融中，diff可见时得分更高。

Conclusion: 工作区状态应被视为知识工作智能体的一个实验变量。现有基准可能因未显式建模工作区状态而低估模型能力，未来基准应将以证据评分、分阶段编辑和提交工件作为显式状态转换来设计。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [203] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HLSR，一种选择性的混合实时—预测车辆重路由框架，在有限干预范围内融合实时路段速度与短期预测，用于缓解城市交通拥堵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络范围内的实时最短路径重路由虽然在仿真中有效，但假设所有车辆在每个决策周期都被重新规划，这在实际中不现实。需要一种在有限干预下仍能有效缓解拥堵的重路由方法。

Method: HLSR框架建立在双阈值拥堵检测、校准的上游车辆选择、面向驾驶员定制的旅行时间预测之上，并进一步引入接近车辆扩展、旅行时间加权的k最短路径生成，以及依赖预测时域混合实时—预测路段速度的多成本路径分配。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验数值结果，但框架设计旨在减少干预范围的同时保持重路由有效性。

Conclusion: HLSR通过选择性干预策略，在有限车辆重规划范围内融合实时与预测信息，为城市交通重路由提供了一种更实际可行的方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [204] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对大型婚恋平台的两项大规模调查（N=2,894和N=2,617，两种语言），构建了基于等级反应模型与潜在回归的"智能体接受度"测量框架，发现发送与接收智能体通信的意愿是可分离但高度相关的构念（ρ=0.92），并量化了"委托不对称"现象：用户部署自己的智能体门槛远低于与对方智能体互动。随机配对反事实显示仅4-13%的有向二元组结合了部署与接收，性别方向失衡显著。设计反事实进一步揭示，基于接收意愿路由智能体联系可使单次参与提升3.1倍（AUC 0.88，经样本外验证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体代表用户对话日益成为匹配平台的新兴设计范式，但其可行性依赖于一个很少被检验的前提：用户不仅需要接受委托对话给智能体，还必须接受来自他人的智能体中介通信。研究旨在系统检验这一关键条件，填补现有文献中智能体接受度测量的空白。

Method: 采用两阶段大规模调查设计（生成式个人资料特征N=2,894；自主对话智能体N=2,617，双语施测）。开发基于等级反应模型（graded response model）与潜在回归的隐变量测量模型，通过模型比较检验发送/接收意愿的构念区分性；利用随机配对反事实和设计反事实（互惠要求、接收意愿路由）量化各种机制的效应，并进行保留目标项的样本外验证。

Result: 发送与接收意愿是高度相关（ρ=0.92）但可分离的构念（ΔBIC=52），存在部分跨语言测量不变性。委托不对称显著：自我部署阈值-0.38 vs. 对方智能体参与阈值+0.32（完全参与+1.39），部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍。随机配对下仅4-13%的有向配对组合部署与接收，且存在性别方向失衡。互惠要求将交互量削减一半以上（排除近三分之二的潜在部署）；基于接收意愿的路由使单次联系参与提升3.1倍（AUC 0.88），样本外验证稳健。

Conclusion: 智能体匹配系统的成功不仅取决于用户是否愿意使用自己的智能体，更取决于他们是否愿意接收他人的智能体通信——而后者是显著更高的门槛。研究为智能体推荐系统设计提供了实证基础，强调需考虑披露机制、选择加入（opt-in）机制以及接受度感知的匹配策略。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [RADmesh: Remesh-Aware Mesh Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17182)
*Nam Anh Dinh,Itai Lang,Oded Stein,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合重网格化的网格变形方法（RADMesh），通过周期性各向同性重网格化配合顶点级变形优化量，解决了基于视觉损失（如文本引导）的生成式形变中因网格拓扑固定导致的单元质量退化问题，同时支持粗到细的局部/全局变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本、视觉监督的生成式网格变形方法大多保持三角剖分不变，大规模几何形变会严重损害单元质量（拉长、扭曲、退化）。重网格化虽能改善单元质量，但作为离散操作难以与视觉损失这类噪声监督信号稳定耦合。

Method: 1) 提出一种顶点级变形优化量，可承受大幅度形变且对视觉损失噪声鲁棒；2) 周期性使用各向同性重网格化器，对变形优化状态进行插值并向前传递，从而在粗细分辨率上持续进行几何感知的优化；3) 支持局部化变形，能在基础形状上生长细节而不影响其余区域。

Result: 在多种形状与文本提示（包括局部与全局变形）上验证了方法的有效性，展现出更优的视觉质量和三角形效率，且重网格化后的三角剖分贴合优化几何并具有规整的各向同性元素。

Conclusion: 通过在变形过程中引入周期性重网格化并保持优化状态的连续性，可在生成式形变中获得高质量、规整的网格，同时兼具局部细节表达能力，为基于视觉损失的网格变形提供了一种稳健且高效的范式。

Abstract: We propose a remeshing-enhanced method for generatively deforming shapes with visual losses. It is intuitive that sufficiently drastic deformations of a mesh without changing its triangulation can easily compromise element quality, even if such large geometry changes may be semantically desired. Shape deformation methods could thus benefit from changing the triangulation; however, this is not done by most generative, text-based, visually-supervised mesh deformation methods. Remeshing is a discrete operation, proven to be especially challenging to couple with the notoriously noisy supervision signal provided by visual losses. We propose a vertex-based deformation optimization quantity capable of large deformations and robustness to such noise; we periodically remesh using an isotropic remesher that interpolates and carries forward the deformation optimization state. This enables continuous, geometry-informed progress in coarse-to-fine addition of resolution. The resulting shapes' triangulations fit their optimized geometry and have neat isotropic elements. Further, our method is localizable, able to grow new features on a base shape with expressive detail, leaving the rest unchanged. We showcase the effectiveness of our method on a variety of shapes and prompts, both local and global deformations, and demonstrate its superior visual quality and triangle efficiency. Our project page is at https://threedle.github.io/radmesh.

</details>


### [206] [3D Gaussian Accelerated Ray Tracing: Fast training through particle-based backward propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17298)
*Laurent Vit,Oliver Batchelor,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 3DGART 是一个面向光线追踪高斯渲染的实用训练框架，通过将反向传播从像素为中心重组为以图元为中心，将梯度计算从高竞争的散射操作转变为结构化的聚集操作，在 Mip-NeRF 360 上实现约 3-3.5 倍（较逐像素基线）和约 4 倍（较 3DGRT）的训练加速，同时提升渲染质量，使全光线追踪高斯训练的运行时间可与光栅化管线竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）虽然通过光栅化实现了高效的实时新视角合成，但其基于屏幕空间的近似限制了准确的视图相关排序以及反射、折射、阴影等二次光线效果的集成。高斯光线追踪通过显式求交解决这些问题，但其训练成本高昂——主要瓶颈并非光线遍历本身，而是基于像素的反向传播中多个线程并发向同一图元参数累加梯度所导致的严重原子竞争和线程串行化。

Method: 3DGART 的核心思想是将反向传播的组织方式从以像素为中心重组为以图元为中心。具体地，利用保守的透视校正屏幕空间边界，构建紧凑的中间缓冲区和 tile-图元映射，使每个线程负责一个图元在某个 tile 覆盖像素上的梯度贡献累加，从而将梯度计算从高竞争的散射（scatter）操作转换为结构化的聚集（gather）式过程。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 数据集上，3DGART 相对于逐像素基线实现了约 3-3.5 倍的原始训练加速，相对于 3DGRT 实现了约 4 倍的加速，同时图像质量还有所提升。此外，全光线追踪高斯训练的运行时间已接近光栅化管线，使射线追踪的优势得以保留。

Conclusion: 3DGART 通过图元中心化的反向传播策略有效消除了光线追踪高斯渲染训练中的原子竞争瓶颈，使得完全基于光线追踪的高斯训练变得实用，在保持光线追踪带来的渲染品质优势的同时，训练速度可与光栅化方法相当。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has made Gaussian primitives a highly efficient representation for real-time novel view synthesis, but its rasterisation-based formulation relies on screen-space approximations that limit accurate view-dependent ordering and the integration of secondary ray effects such as reflections, refractions, and shadows. Gaussian ray tracing addresses these limitations by evaluating explicit ray-primitive intersections, yet it remains costly to train. We observe that the main bottleneck is not ray traversal alone, but the pixel-centric backward propagation, where many threads concurrently accumulate gradients into the same primitive parameters, causing severe atomic contention and thread serialisation.
  We present 3DGART, a practical training framework for ray-traced Gaussian rendering. Our key idea is to reorganise backward propagation around primitives rather than pixels. Using conservative perspective-correct screen-space bounds, we build a compact intermediate buffer and a tile-primitive mapping that allows each thread to accumulate the contribution of one primitive over its covered pixels within a tile. This transforms gradient computation from a contention-heavy scatter operation into a structured gather-like process. On Mip-NeRF 360, 3DGART achieves an $\approx 3-3.5\times$ raw training speedup over per-pixel baseline and $\approx4 \times$ over 3DGRT on Mip-NeRF 360 while improving quality. More importantly, 3DGART makes fully ray-traced Gaussian training practical, reaching runtimes competitive with rasterisation-based pipelines while preserving benefits of ray tracing.

</details>


### [207] [Six Ways to Draw Vangers with WebGPU: Real-Time Rendering of Editable Multi-Layer Height Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17390)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文比较了六种地形渲染方法（高度场光线步进、体素加速光线步进、切片代理几何、逐采样条栅格化、计算散射和拟合三角网格），在Vangers（1998）手工制作的多层地形上，以CPU光线投射为基准进行对比评估。所有方法需保留地面样本的两个实心间隔、以交互帧率渲染并支持局部地形破坏而无需重载关卡。结果表明贪心TIN在帧时表现最优，但几何拟合成本和显存开销较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地形LOD的评估几乎完全基于数字高程模型（单值、采样尺度平滑、由真实地形采样），但游戏地形通常不具备这些特性，尤其是多层地形（如带洞穴的地形）。作者希望为这些复杂游戏地形上的渲染方法提供一个公平、统一的比较基准。

Method: 在同一引擎、同一数据路径下实现并比较六种渲染方法：高度场光线步进、体素加速光线步进、切片代理几何、逐样本条栅格化、计算散射和拟合三角网格（贪心TIN）。以Vangers（1998）的手工多层地形为测试数据，以同源数据的CPU光线投射作为基准。所有方法基于wgpu/WebGPU统一实现，遵循三个约束条件：保留两个实心间隔、交互帧率、支持局部地形破坏无需重载。

Result: 在选定的质量设置下，贪心TIN在所有测试设备上具有最低的平均帧时，但其拟合成本由第二层（而非地表起伏）决定，且网格可编辑性需要319 MiB GPU几何和535 MiB CPU三角化存储。顶部相机视角下六种方法几乎无法区分，但在视平线视角下，点散射丢失覆盖率、切片产生条带化伪影、过度简化的网格可能漏掉墙面。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传统DEM假设与游戏真实地形需求之间的差距，为多层地形渲染方法的选择提供了量化参考。贪心TIN在性能上最优但存储和编辑成本高，其他方法各有取舍。所有代码和测量协议已开源。

Abstract: Terrain level-of-detail is measured almost exclusively on digital elevation models: single-valued, smooth at the sampling scale, sampled from real topography. Game terrain is often none of these. We compare six rendering methods - height-field ray marching, voxel-accelerated ray marching, sliced proxy geometry, per-sample bar rasterization, compute scattering, and a fitted triangle mesh - implemented in a single engine over a single data path, on the hand-authored multi-layer terrain of Vangers (1998), scored against a CPU ray cast of the same source data. Every method must preserve the two solid intervals available at a ground sample, render at interactive rates, and reflect local terrain destruction without reloading the level. These constraints rule out treating caves as decoration or amortizing a static preprocessing step over an immutable map.
  From the original game's top-down camera the six methods look interchangeable. At eye-level horizons they do not: point scattering loses coverage, slicing bands, and an over-simplified mesh can miss a wall. At the selected quality settings a greedy triangulated irregular network (TIN) has the lowest mean frame time on every device we measured, but the fit cost is set by the second layer rather than by floor relief, and making that mesh editable retains 319 MiB of GPU geometry and 535 MiB of CPU triangulation. All six implementations use the same native wgpu / WebGPU API and canonical WGSL. We release the engine, the harness, and a one-command measurement protocol.

</details>


### [208] [Variational r-Adaptive Cloth Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17833)
*Jiahao Wen,Zhen Chen,Jernej Barbič,Danny M. Kaufman*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向布料动力学与静力学模拟的r自适应方法，结合摩擦接触，通过退化激活的质量正则化解决现有变分r自适应在分段线性壳上的局部极小值陷阱和单元坍塌问题，并引入动态非线性求解器实现3-6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄布料需要高有效空间分辨率以再现褶皱、折叠、屈曲和尖锐接触特征，但现有变分r自适应应用于分段线性壳时存在两个耦合失败模式：离散化增量势能（IP）优化易陷入差的局部极小值，以及元素坍塌导致有限元近似失效。

Method: 提出退化激活质量正则化（degeneracy-activated quality regularization），对形状良好的单元保持非激活以保留各向异性自适应和局部加密，但在接近退化时增强以抑制虚假低能盆地、改善从次优物理极小值逃逸，并防止元素聚集（布料在尖锐接触特征上滑动时元素逐渐坍塌的特有失效）。此外引入动态非线性求解器，利用帧内时间连贯性通过加速导数求值和动态IPC容差更新来加速r自适应迭代信任域（ITR）求解。

Result: 在挑战性摩擦接触场景下，与固定网格相比，在相同顶点数和时间预算约束下，该方法实现了更高的视觉保真度，且相比先前的优化ITR获得3-6倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作在保持自适应网格优势的同时，通过针对性正则化克服了r自适应在布料接触模拟中的关键数值失效，并大幅提升了实际求解效率，验证了其在现代布料流水线中的实用性。

Abstract: We present the first r-adaptive method for simulating cloth dynamics and statics with frictional contact in modern cloth pipelines. Thin cloth requires high effective spatial resolution to reproduce wrinkles, folds, buckling, and sharp contact features. However, applying existing variational r-adaptivity to piecewise-linear shells reveals two coupled failure modes. Discretized incremental-potential (IP) optimization can become trapped in poor local minima, yielding suboptimal physical configurations. It can also lower IP artificially by collapsing elements, invalidating the finite-element approximation on which the objective relies. We address both problems with degeneracy-activated quality regularization. The regularizer remains inactive for well-shaped elements, preserving anisotropic adaptation and local densification, but becomes strong near degeneracy. It suppresses spurious low-energy basins, improves escape from suboptimal physical minima, and prevents element bunching, a cloth-specific failure in which elements progressively collapse as cloth slides across sharp contact features. For practical performance, we introduce a dynamic nonlinear solver that exploits within-timestep coherence through accelerated derivative evaluation and dynamic IPC tolerance updates for r-adaptive iterative trust-region (ITR) solves. This yields a 3-6x speedup over prior optimal ITR. Experiments on challenging frictional-contact scenarios show that, under equal vertex-count and time-budget constraints, our method achieves higher visual fidelity than fixed meshes.

</details>


### [209] [Love Handles: Decimation for Deformation Handles with Compact Support and Low Memory Footprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17930)
*David IW Levin,Paul Kry,Kartic Subr,Ryan Schmidt,Etienne Vouga,Teseo Schneider*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于简化（decimation）的算法，用于计算稀疏、紧凑支撑的变形手柄（handles），以加速弹性固体的降阶模拟。该方法通过迭代代数简化优化手柄变形以匹配任意输入变形，适用于任意体积网格（包括高亏格或多孔结构），并设计了高效的紧凑支撑权重更新算法和降阶立方体求积方案，在多达796,623个四面体的网格上实现了实时弹性动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体变形的物理模拟在计算机动画、工程和机器人领域具有重要意义，但传统有限元模拟计算开销大，且随离散化程度提高而扩展性差。降阶方法（ROM）通过减少自由度来节省计算，例如使用控制顶点组的手柄，但现有的手柄生成方法缺乏稀疏性和紧凑性，且难以处理复杂几何。本文旨在提出一种高效算法，能够自动生成稀疏、紧凑支撑的变形手柄，从而在保持精度的同时实现大规模复杂网格的实时模拟。

Method: 本文提出了第一个基于简化的手柄计算算法。核心方法包括：(1) 利用迭代代数简化手段优化手柄变形以匹配任意输入变形（如线性振动模态）；(2) 该方法适用于任意体积输入网格，包括高亏格或多孔特征，因为不改变几何形状；(3) 设计了高效算法来计算和更新紧凑支撑及其相关权重；(4) 利用紧凑支撑开发了高效的降阶立方体（reduced-cubature）求积计算方案。

Result: 优化后的手柄在提供内存高效解决方案的同时，能够实现对复杂几何的实时弹性动力学模拟。文章展示了在多种四面体网格上的实时性能，最高支持796,623个四面体。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种生成稀疏紧凑支撑变形手柄的新方法，显著减少了降阶模拟的内存与计算开销，使高分辨率复杂几何网格的实时弹性动力学模拟成为可能，在动画、工程和机器人等应用场景中具有广泛的实际价值。

Abstract: Estimating the deformation of solids via physical simulation is an important problem spanning fields such as computer animation, engineering and robotics. Such simulations are computationally expensive and scale poorly when the representation of an object is refined by increasing the level of discretization. Reduced Order Methods (ROM) offer computational savings by decreasing the number of degrees of freedom, for example by using \emph{handles} that control groups of vertices. We present the first decimation-based algorithm for computing a sparse, compactly supported set of deformation handles. The crux of our method utilizes iterative algebraic simplification to optimize handle deformation to match any input deformation, such as linear vibration modes. This applies to any volumetric input mesh, including those with high genus or porous features, since we do not alter the geometry. We also devise an efficient algorithm to compute and update compact supports and their associated weights. We leverage compact support to develop an efficient, reduced-cubature computation scheme. Once optimized, our handles offer a memory-efficient solution while enabling real-time elastodynamics simulation of complex geometry. We show real-time performance on a variety of tetrahedral meshes with up to 796,623 tetrahedra.

</details>


### [210] [MetaSapiens v2: Advancing Real-Time Foveated Neural Rendering via Foveation-Aware Pruning and Stereo Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17969)
*Weikai Lin,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MetaSapiens v2是一个面向VR/AR设备的实时点基神经渲染（PBNR）系统，通过四种技术（效率感知剪枝、注视点渲染、选择性双目warping和双目FR加速器）实现较现有PBNR模型一个数量级的加速，同时保持人类视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBNR是一项有前景的渲染技术，广泛应用于AR/VR和数字孪生等领域，但在VR/AR设备上实现实时渲染面临巨大挑战，需要在保证视觉质量的同时大幅提升渲染速度。

Method: MetaSapiens v2结合四种关键技术：(1) 效率感知的剪枝技术优化渲染速度；(2) 利用人类周边视觉低敏锐度的特点，提出面向PBNR的高效原语的注视点渲染（FR）方法，在周围区域降低渲染质量以提升速度；(3) 利用双眼之间的冗余信息，提出选择性warping方法降低AR/VR双目渲染的计算开销；(4) 设计面向双目FR的加速器架构，解决基于FR的PBNR负载不均衡问题，并支持warping实现高效双目渲染。

Result: 实验评估表明，MetaSapiens v2相比现有PBNR模型实现了约一个数量级的加速，同时保持了视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过软件与硬件协同设计，MetaSapiens v2成功证明了在VR/AR设备上实现实时、高质量PBNR渲染的可行性，为神经渲染技术在沉浸式终端的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Point-Based Neural Rendering (PBNR) is emerging as a promising class of rendering techniques, which are permeating all aspects of society, driven by a growing demand for real-time, photorealistic rendering in AR/VR and digital twins. However, achieving real-time PBNR on VR/AR devices is challenging. This paper proposes MetaSapiens v2, a PBNR system that delivers real-time neural rendering on VR/AR devices while maintaining human visual quality. MetaSapiens v2 combines four techniques. First, we present an efficiency-aware pruning technique to optimize rendering speed. Second, we introduce a Foveated Rendering (FR) method with an efficient primitive for PBNR, leveraging humans' low visual acuity in peripheral regions to relax rendering quality and improve rendering speed. Third, we leverage the redundancy between the two eyes and propose a selective warping method to further reduce the computation overhead in AR/VR binocular rendering. Finally, we propose an accelerator design for binocular FR, addressing the load imbalance issue in (FR-based) PBNR and supporting warping for efficient binocular rendering. Our evaluation shows that MetaSapiens v2 achieves an order of magnitude speedup over existing PBNR models while maintaining the visual quality.

</details>


### [211] [aDSL: Agentic 3D Creation via Joint Agent-Program Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17975)
*Rui-Huan Wang,Si-Tong Wei,Jia-Qi He,Heng-Yi Wei,Baoquan Chen,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Agent-centric Domain-Specific Language (aDSL)与角色专业化的多智能体系统协同设计框架，通过关系运算符代替脆弱的绝对坐标，配合Plan-Execute-Critic循环，解决LLM在3D程序化生成中高层面意图与低级几何不一致的问题，在文本转形状和图像转形状任务上超越了现有LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的3D程序化生成工作流往往脆弱，难以将高层面意图转化为一致的底层几何。作者将其归因于现有程序化接口与LLM推理能力之间的不匹配——LLM擅长语义结构和空间关系，而不擅长脆弱的数值选择。因此需要重新设计DSL和智能体架构，使其与LLM的优势对齐。

Method: 提出aDSL（Agent-centric Domain-Specific Language），强调可组合性和空间推理，使智能体通过关系运算符而非绝对坐标操控几何。在此基础上，构建一个无需训练的多智能体系统，采用Plan-Execute-Critic循环：分解请求、合成代码、利用执行反馈迭代修复错误和约束违规。

Result: 实验表明，该协同设计方法在文本转形状和图像转形状任务上优于先前的LLM基线，同时保持显式结构、可编辑性和可解释性。此外还支持下游应用，如关节物体创建和结构化场景组合。

Conclusion: 通过DSL与多智能体系统的联合设计（co-design），可以有效弥合LLM语义推理与3D几何生成之间的鸿沟，显著提升鲁棒性、可控性和对用户意图的忠实度。

Abstract: Programmatic representations provide a compelling paradigm for 3D content creation, enabling fine-grained edits, interpretability, and explicit structural control. Yet, agentic workflows that rely on large language models (LLMs) to author 3D programs remain brittle, often failing to translate high-level intent into consistent low-level geometry. We attribute this fragility to a mismatch between existing programmatic interfaces and the reasoning strengths of LLMs, which favor semantic structure and spatial relations over fragile numeric choices. In this paper, we jointly design an Agent-centric Domain-Specific Language (aDSL) and a role-specialized multi-agent system to close this gap. aDSL bridges semantic logic and geometric constraints by emphasizing composability and spatial reasoning; it enables agents to manipulate geometry through relational operators instead of brittle absolute coordinates. Building on aDSL, our training-free multi-agent system follows a Plan-Execute-Critic loop to decompose requests, synthesize code, and iteratively repair errors and constraint violations using execution feedback. Experiments show that this co-design improves robustness, controllability, and faithfulness to user intent. Our method outperforms prior LLM-based baselines on text-to-shape and image-to-shape tasks while preserving explicit structure, editability, and interpretability. It also enables downstream applications such as articulated object creation and structured scene composition. Our code is available at https://github.com/sig-pku/aDSL.

</details>
